//! Immutable default rollback views, authorized by a committed parent deploy.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — registration shares the deployment's commit boundary.
use super::Project;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::{
    fs,
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
};
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(super) struct View {
    pub target: u64,
    pub release: u64,
    pub parent: u64,
    pub digest: String,
    pub changes: Vec<String>,
    pub hides: Vec<String>,
}
pub(super) fn is_view(dir: &Path, n: u64) -> bool {
    dir.join("releases")
        .join(n.to_string())
        .join("rollback-view.json")
        .is_file()
}
pub(super) fn metadata(root: &Path) -> Result<Option<View>, String> {
    match fs::read(root.join("rollback-view.json")) {
        Ok(bytes) => serde_json::from_slice(&bytes)
            .map(Some)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(e) => Err(e.to_string()),
    }
}
pub(super) fn manifest(root: &Path) -> Result<Vec<View>, String> {
    match fs::read(root.join("rollback-views.json")) {
        Ok(bytes) => serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(Vec::new()),
        Err(e) => Err(e.to_string()),
    }
}
pub(super) fn resolve(dir:&Path,target:u64)->Result<Option<View>,String>{
    let store=snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let Some(active)=store.release_value().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.map(|s|s.parse::<u64>().map_err(|e|e.to_string())).transpose()?else{return Ok(None)};
    drop(store);
    let root=dir.join("releases").join(active.to_string());
    let context=if let Some(view)=metadata(&root)?{verify(dir,&view)?;view.parent}else{active};
    super::releases::selectable(dir,context)?;
    let view=manifest(&dir.join("releases").join(context.to_string()))?.into_iter().find(|v|v.target==target);
    if let Some(view)=&view{verify(dir,view)?;}Ok(view)
}
pub(super) fn verify(dir: &Path, view: &View) -> Result<(), String> {
    if view.parent == view.release || is_view(dir, view.parent) {
        return Err("invalid rollback view parent".into());
    }
    super::releases::selectable(dir, view.parent)?;
    let parent = dir.join("releases").join(view.parent.to_string());
    if !manifest(&parent)?.iter().any(|v| v == view) {
        return Err("rollback view is not registered by its committed parent".into());
    }
    let root = dir.join("releases").join(view.release.to_string());
    if metadata(&root)?.as_ref()!=Some(view){return Err("rollback view metadata differs from its committed manifest".into())}
    if crate::release_files::executable_digest(&root)? != view.digest {
        return Err("rollback view source digest changed".into());
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn prepare(
    dir: &Path,
    stage: &Path,
    bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound,
    parent: u64,
) -> Result<Vec<View>, String> {
    let current = Project { root: stage.into() }
        .sources()
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut views = Vec::new();
    let mut id = parent;
    for target in super::releases::ordinary_releases(dir)? {
        let old_root = dir.join("releases").join(target.to_string());
        let old = super::releases::bound_at(old_root.clone())?;
        let original=Project{root:old_root.clone()}.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        let risks = super::rollback_foresight::risks(&bound.schema, &old.schema, target, dir);
        let widening=snapback4_lang::release_bridge::requires_view(&original,&current);
        let defaulting=super::rollback_foresight::missing_defaults(&bound.schema,&old.schema)?;
        if !defaulting.is_empty(){return Err(format!("E_ADOPT: rollback to release {target} would misrepresent a removed defaulted column {}; declare a truthful cross-table down before deploy: {}",defaulting.join(", "),risks.join("; ")))}
        // Quarry text is only part of the authored execution closure. A worker
        // or native-only repair must not restore an older executable behind
        // unchanged intent declarations. Metadata-only registration is excluded
        // by the shared executable digest, keeping equal-source no-ops finite.
        let closure_changed = !bound.invariants.is_empty()
            && snapback4_lang::release_bridge::same_data_and_intent(&original,&current)
            && crate::release_files::executable_digest(&old_root)?
                != crate::release_files::executable_digest(stage)?;
        let visibility_risks=super::rollback_foresight::old_row_risks(&bound.schema,&old.schema,target,dir);
        if !widening && !closure_changed && visibility_risks.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        if crate::dev_worker::source(&Project {root:stage.into()}).is_some()
            || !crate::dev_worker::declared(&Project {root:stage.into()})?.is_empty()
            || crate::dev_worker::source(&Project {
            root: old_root.clone(),
        })
        .is_some()
            || !crate::dev_worker::declared(&Project {
                root: old_root.clone(),
            })?
            .is_empty()
        {
            return Err("E_ADOPT: a generated rollback view needs an authored worker bundle; retain a compatible guarded source".into());
        }
        let hides = bound
            .rollback_hides
            .iter()
            .filter(|h| h.release == target)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let mut source = current.clone();
        // A typed null acknowledgment retains this one old nonnullable domain;
        // all other widened columns still receive the visible bridge union.
        for hide in &hides {
            let old_kind = old
                .schema
                .table(&hide.table)
                .and_then(|t| t.columns.get(&hide.column))
                .ok_or("rollback hides names no predecessor column")?;
            if bound.schema.table_renames.contains_key(&hide.table)
                || bound
                    .schema
                    .asset_renames
                    .get(&hide.table)
                    .is_some_and(|m| m.contains_key(&hide.column))
            {
                return Err("rollback hides requires the same table/column spelling in the original release".into());
            }
            if old_kind.is_optional() {
                return Err("rollback hides requires a nonnullable predecessor column".into());
            }
            for (file, text) in &mut source {
                let doc = snapback4_lang::parse_document(file, text).0;
                if let Some(column) = doc
                    .tables
                    .iter()
                    .find(|t| t.name == hide.table)
                    .and_then(|t| t.columns.iter().find(|c| c.name == hide.column))
                {
                    if let Some((start, end)) =
                        snapback4_lang::release_bridge::type_span(file, text, column)
                    {
                        let scalar = column.type_.required();
                        text.replace_range(
                            start..end,
                            &snapback4_lang::print::scalar_type(&scalar),
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        let bridge = snapback4_lang::release_bridge::loosen(
            &original,
            &source,
        );
        if !bridge.diagnostics.is_empty() {
            return Err(format!(
                "E_ADOPT: rollback view for release {target} does not compile; retain a truthful guarded old workflow or declare its intentional hiding: {}",
                bridge
                    .diagnostics
                    .iter()
                    .map(ToString::to_string)
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join("; ")
            ));
        }
        let compiled = snapback4_lang::compile(&bridge.sources);
        let mut aliases:std::collections::BTreeMap<String,String> = bound
            .schema
            .table_renames
            .iter()
            .filter(|(_, old)| compiled.schema.tables.contains_key(*old))
            .map(|(new, old)| (new.clone(), old.clone()))
            .collect();
        for(new,prior)in &compiled.schema.table_renames{if bound.schema.tables.contains_key(prior)&&!bound.schema.tables.contains_key(new){aliases.insert(prior.clone(),new.clone());}}
        let current = bound
            .schema
            .with_table_names(&aliases)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let mut columns=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
        let view_docs=bridge.sources.iter().map(|(file,text)|snapback4_lang::parse_document(file,text).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let current_docs=source.iter().map(|(file,text)|snapback4_lang::parse_document(file,text).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — source transport and hard constraints must agree on scalar as well as reference identities.
        for name in compiled.schema.tables.keys(){if let Some(next)=current.table(name){
            let prior=view_docs.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables).find(|t|&t.name==name);
            let authored=current_docs.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables).find(|t|aliases.get(&t.name).unwrap_or(&t.name)==name);
            if let(Some(prior),Some(authored))=(prior,authored){
                let map=snapback4_lang::release_bridge::declared_column_names(prior,authored)
                    .ok_or_else(||format!("E_ADOPT: rollback view for release {target} needs unambiguous column identities on {name}; author a compatible guarded rollback workflow"))?;
                if next.columns.keys().map(|c|map.get(c).unwrap_or(c)).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>().len()!=next.columns.len(){
                    return Err(format!("E_ADOPT: rollback view for release {target} cannot merge independent column identities on {name}; author a compatible guarded rollback workflow"));
                }
                if !map.is_empty(){columns.insert(name.clone(),map);}
            }
        }}
        let current=current.with_column_names(&columns);
        for (name, table) in &compiled.schema.tables {
            if let Some(next) = current.table(name) {
                use snapback4_core::schema::Constraint;
                let hard=|c:&Constraint|!matches!(c,Constraint::Text{..}|Constraint::Unique{..}|Constraint::Capabilities(_)|Constraint::Recorded);
                let column=|field:&String|field.clone();
                for c in next.constraints.iter().filter(|c|hard(c)) {
                    let mut constraint=c.clone();
                    match &mut constraint {Constraint::Cap{by,..}|Constraint::Immutable{fields:by}=>for f in by{*f=column(f)},_=>()}
                    if !table.constraints.contains(&constraint){return Err(format!("E_ADOPT: rollback view for release {target} cannot preserve current constraint on {name}; author a compatible guarded rollback workflow"))}
                }
                for index in next.indexes.values().filter(|i|i.unique){
                    let mut components=index.components.clone();for part in &mut components{if let snapback4_core::schema::IndexComponent::Column(c)=part{*c=column(c);}}
                    if !table.indexes.values().any(|i|i.unique&&i.components==components){return Err(format!("E_ADOPT: rollback view for release {target} cannot preserve a current unique key on {name}; author a compatible guarded rollback workflow"))}
                }
                let on_delete=next.on_delete.iter().map(|(field,policy)|(column(field),policy.clone())).collect::<std::collections::BTreeMap<_,_>>();
                if table.expire!=next.expire||table.not_before!=next.not_before||table.through!=next.through||table.on_delete!=on_delete||table.sync!=next.sync||table.leave!=next.leave {
                    return Err(format!("E_ADOPT: rollback view for release {target} cannot preserve current lifetime or sync audience on {name}; author a compatible guarded rollback workflow"))
                }
                if ![
                    (&table.rules.read, &next.rules.read),
                    (&table.rules.insert, &next.rules.insert),
                    (&table.rules.update, &next.rules.update),
                    (&table.rules.delete, &next.rules.delete),
                ]
                .iter()
                .all(|(view, current)| view == current)
                    || table.columns.keys().any(|c| {
                        table.column_reads.get(c) != next.column_reads.get(c)
                            || table.written_by.get(c) != next.written_by.get(c)
                    })
                {
                    return Err(format!(
                        "E_ADOPT: rollback view for release {target} cannot preserve current authority on {name}; retain an authored compatible guarded old workflow before deploying this widening"
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
        if compiled.programs.iter().any(|p| p.program.native) {
            return Err("E_ADOPT: a generated rollback view needs an authored native bundle; retain a compatible guarded source".into());
        }
        id = id.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?;
        let root = stage.join("rollback-views").join(id.to_string());
        fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        for (name, text) in &bridge.sources {
            let path = root.join("snapback").join(name);
            if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
                fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
            fs::write(path, text).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        }
        let mut view = View {
            target,
            release: id,
            parent,
            digest: String::new(),
            changes: bridge.changes,
            hides: hides
                .iter()
                .map(|h| format!("{} where {} = null because {}", h.table, h.column, h.reason))
                .collect(),
        };
        fs::write(
            root.join("device-compatibility.json"),
            serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(
                &compiled.schema.public_projection(),
            ))
            .unwrap(),
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        view.digest = crate::release_files::executable_digest(&root)?;
        fs::write(
            root.join("rollback-view.json"),
            serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&view).unwrap(),
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        views.push(view);
    }
    fs::write(
        stage.join("rollback-views.json"),
        serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&views).unwrap(),
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    Ok(views)
}
pub(super) struct Installed {
    dir: PathBuf,
    parent: u64,
    ids: Vec<u64>,
    pub pending: bool,
}
impl Drop for Installed {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if !self.pending && super::releases::selectable(&self.dir, self.parent).is_err() {
            for id in &self.ids {
                let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(self.dir.join("releases").join(id.to_string()));
            }
        }
    }
}
pub(super) fn install(
    dir: &Path,
    stage: &Path,
    parent: u64,
    views: &[View],
) -> Result<Installed, String> {
    let mut installed = Installed {
        dir: dir.into(),
        parent,
        ids: Vec::new(),
        pending: false,
    };
    for v in views {
        let from = stage.join("rollback-views").join(v.release.to_string());
        crate::release_files::durable(&from)?;
        fs::rename(&from, dir.join("releases").join(v.release.to_string()))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        installed.ids.push(v.release);
    }
    if stage.join("rollback-views").exists() {
        fs::remove_dir(stage.join("rollback-views")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    Ok(installed)
}

pub(super) fn needs_registration(dir:&Path,matched:Option<u64>,prepared:&[View])->Result<bool,String>{
    let held=matched.map(|n|manifest(&dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))).transpose()?.unwrap_or_default();
    for view in &held{verify(dir,view)?;}
    Ok(held.len()!=prepared.len()||prepared.iter().any(|v|!held.iter().any(|old|old.target==v.target&&old.digest==v.digest&&old.hides==v.hides)))
}

//! Independent policy follows the complete authored current execution closure.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — no restored workflow may bypass current independent intent.
use crate::{
    ast::{self, TypeKind},
    lexer::{self, Kind},
    parser, print,
    release_bridge::Bridge,
};
use snapback4_core::ir::ArgType;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

type Source = (String, String);

pub(super) fn prepare(target: &[Source], current: &[Source], guarded_fallback: &mut bool) -> Bridge {
    *guarded_fallback=false;
    let old = crate::compile(target);
    let next = crate::compile(current);
    if !old.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Bridge {
            sources: target.to_vec(),
            changes: vec![],
            diagnostics: old.diagnostics,
        };
    }
    if !next.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Bridge {
            sources: current.to_vec(),
            changes: vec![],
            diagnostics: next.diagnostics,
        };
    }
    if contracts(&old, &next) {
        return retained(current.to_vec(), false);
    }
    match alpha(target, current) {
        Ok(Some(sources)) => {
            let bound = crate::compile(&sources);
            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
                return Bridge {
                    sources,
                    changes: vec![],
                    diagnostics: bound.diagnostics,
                };
            }
            if contracts(&old, &bound) {
                return retained(sources, true);
            }
        }
        Err(error) => {*guarded_fallback=error.unsupported;return refused(current, &error.reason)},
        Ok(None) => {}
    }
    *guarded_fallback=true;
    refused(current,"the complete current workflow does not retain every predecessor table, field, operation and accepted input")
}
fn retained(sources: Vec<Source>, alpha: bool) -> Bridge {
    Bridge {sources,diagnostics:vec![],changes:vec![format!("current independent intent: retained the complete current authored schema, defaults, programs, helpers, jobs and policy declarations{}; predecessor names and accepted inputs remain available; review current results in the old UI",if alpha {" under declared table identity renames"}else{""})]}
}
fn refused(current: &[Source], reason: &str) -> Bridge {
    Bridge {sources:current.to_vec(),changes:vec![],diagnostics:vec![crate::diag::type_error(&crate::Site::default(),format!("rollback view cannot preserve current independent intent: {reason}"),"author a compatible guarded source retaining the predecessor fields and inputs, truthful migrations and the current policy; an acknowledgment does not authorize restoring an unsafe workflow").guide("deploy")]}
}

/// A loosened current workflow may omit a predecessor's nullable, default-free
/// field rather than invent its value. All current schema and executable
/// declarations stay intact; old wire aliases are the only source additions.
pub(super) fn guarded(target:&[Source],current:&[Source])->Bridge {
    let normalized=match alpha(target,current) {
        Ok(sources)=>sources,
        Err(error)=>return refused(current,&error.reason),
    };
    let current=normalized.as_deref().unwrap_or(current);
    let old=crate::compile(target);let next=crate::compile(current);
    if !old.diagnostics.is_empty()||!next.diagnostics.is_empty(){return refused(current,"both authored sources must compile before preparing a guarded view");}
    if !table_contracts(&old,&next,true){return refused(current,"the guarded current workflow needs every predecessor table and required/defaulted field; author a truthful reverse projection or compatible guarded source");}
    let mut sources=current.to_vec();let mut changes=vec!["guarded loosened view: retained the complete current schema, defaults, helpers, programs, jobs, aliases and independent intent; omitted nullable default-free predecessor fields are absent results, never invented values; review current results in the old UI".into()];
    if normalized.is_some(){changes.push("declared table identities: complete current guarded workflow retained under predecessor spellings, without rewriting business strings or weakening authority".into());}
    for(file,text)in &mut sources {
        let doc=parser::parse_document(file,text).0;
        let Some((prior_file,prior_text))=target.iter().find(|(f,_)|super::release_bridge::operation_name(f,"")==super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,""))else{continue};
        let prior=parser::parse_document(prior_file,prior_text).0;
        let(tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,text);let mut edits=Vec::new();let mut extra=String::new();let mut restored=BTreeMap::new();
        // Retain a missing old exported alias only to an authored current
        // endpoint (including an explicitly authored current alias target).
        // Existing/rebound current aliases remain exactly current-authored.
        for(name,target,_)in &prior.aliases {
            let qualified=super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,name);
            if next.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name==qualified){continue;}
            let endpoint=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target).or_else(||doc.aliases.iter().find(|(alias,_,_)|alias==target).and_then(|(_,target,_)|doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target)));
            if let Some(endpoint)=endpoint{extra.push_str(&format!("alias {name} = {}\n",endpoint.name));restored.insert(qualified,endpoint.name.clone());}
        }
        for previous in &old.programs {
            let qualified=&previous.program.name;
            let endpoint=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&p.name)==*qualified).or_else(|| {
                let target=doc.aliases.iter().find(|(name,_,_)|super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,name)==*qualified).map(|(_,target,_)|target).or_else(||restored.get(qualified))?;
                doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target)
            });
            let Some(endpoint)=endpoint else{continue};
            let actual=super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&endpoint.name);
            let Some(current)=next.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==*qualified||p.program.name==actual)else{continue};
            for(alias,owner)in &previous.program.arg_aliases {
                if current.program.argument(alias).is_some(){continue;}
                let Some((canonical,_))=current.program.argument(owner)else{continue};
                let Some(arg)=endpoint.args.iter().find(|arg|arg.name==canonical&&arg.alias.is_none())else{continue};
                if let Ok((_,end))=arg_type(text,&tokens,arg){edits.push((end,end,format!(" alias {alias}")));changes.push(format!("operation {qualified}: retained wire alias {alias} on {canonical}"));}
            }
        }
        edits.sort();edits.dedup();edits.sort_by_key(|edit|std::cmp::Reverse(edit.0));
        for(start,end,value)in edits{text.replace_range(start..end,&value);}
        if !extra.is_empty(){if !text.ends_with('\n'){text.push('\n');}text.push_str(&extra);}
    }
    let bound=crate::compile(&sources);
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty(){return Bridge{sources,changes,diagnostics:bound.diagnostics};}
    if !table_contracts(&old,&bound,true)||!endpoint_contracts(&old,&bound,true){return refused(current,"the guarded current workflow cannot retain a predecessor query or a retained export's wire aliases and distinct inputs; author a compatible guarded endpoint with the old accepted arguments");}
    for(name,table)in &old.schema.tables {
        for column in table.columns.keys().filter(|column|!bound.schema.tables[name].columns.contains_key(*column)) {
            if let Some(current)=renamed_column(&old,&bound,name,column){changes.push(format!("{name}.{column}: declared identity is now {name}.{current}; guarded view uses the current spelling and result semantics; review the old UI"));}
            else{changes.push(format!("{name}.{column}: removed nullable default-free field remains absent in this guarded view; historical cells stay retained; review the old UI result"));}
        }
    }
    for previous in &old.programs {
        if !bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name==previous.program.name){changes.push(format!("operation {}: paused because no compatible current authored write endpoint exists; old calls return E_OP; author a guarded compatibility endpoint if this old workflow is still needed",previous.program.name));}
    }
    Bridge{sources,changes,diagnostics:vec![]}
}

pub(super) fn contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound)->bool {
    table_contracts(old,new,false)&&endpoint_contracts(old,new,false)
}
fn table_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,nullable_gaps:bool)->bool {
    old.schema.tables.iter().all(|(name,table)|new.schema.tables.get(name).is_some_and(|next| {
        table.columns.iter().all(|(column,kind)|next.columns.contains_key(column)
            || (nullable_gaps&&renamed_column(old,new,name,column).is_some())
            || (nullable_gaps&&kind.is_optional()&&!table.defaults.contains_key(column)&&!table.engine_field(column)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(column)))
    }))
}
fn endpoint_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,pause_removed_writes:bool)->bool {
    old.programs.iter().all(|p|new.programs.iter().find(|n|n.program.name==p.program.name)
        .map_or_else(||pause_removed_writes&&matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action),|n|endpoint_contract(&p.program,&n.program)))
}
pub(super) fn endpoint_contract(old:&snapback4_core::Program,new:&snapback4_core::Program)->bool {
    if old.kind!=new.kind{return false;}
    let mut positions=BTreeSet::new();
    for(name,kind)in &old.args {
        let Some((target,next))=new.argument(name)else{return false};
        if !accepts(kind,next)||!positions.insert(target){return false;}
        if !old.arg_aliases.iter().filter(|(_,owner)|*owner==name).all(|(alias,_)|new.argument(alias).is_some_and(|(canonical,next)|canonical==target&&accepts(kind,next))){return false;}
    }
    new.args.iter().all(|(name,kind)|positions.contains(name.as_str())||matches!(kind,ArgType::Optional(_)))
}
fn renamed_column<'a>(old:&crate::Bound,new:&'a crate::Bound,table:&str,column:&str)->Option<&'a str> {
    let next=new.schema.table(table)?;
    if let Some((name,_))=new.schema.asset_renames.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|(name,prior)|prior.as_str()==column&&next.columns.contains_key(*name)){return Some(name.as_str());}
    if let Some(prior)=old.schema.asset_renames.get(table).and_then(|m|m.get(column)) {
        if let Some((name,_))=next.columns.get_key_value(prior){return Some(name.as_str());}
    }
    for transform in &new.schema.backfills {
        if transform.table==table&&transform.inputs.len()==1&&transform.inputs.contains_key(column)&&transform.outputs.len()==1 {
            let name=transform.outputs.iter().next()?;
            if transform.name==format!("$rename:up:{column}:{name}")&&next.columns.contains_key(name){return Some(name.as_str());}
        }
    }
    for transform in &old.schema.backfills {
        if transform.table==table&&transform.outputs.len()==1&&transform.outputs.contains(column)&&transform.inputs.len()==1 {
            let prior=transform.inputs.keys().next()?;
            if transform.name==format!("$rename:up:{prior}:{column}") {
                if let Some((name,_))=next.columns.get_key_value(prior){return Some(name.as_str());}
            }
        }
    }
    None
}
// This is inclusion of wire domains for an unchanged authored current body,
// not synthesis of a conversion or permission to reinterpret old arguments.
fn accepts(old: &ArgType, new: &ArgType) -> bool {
    if old == new {
        return true;
    }
    match (old, new) {
        (ArgType::Optional(a), ArgType::Optional(b)) => accepts(a, b),
        (_, ArgType::Optional(b)) => accepts(old, b),
        (ArgType::Optional(_), _) => false,
        (
            ArgType::Text {
                min: a,
                max: b,
                format: f,
            },
            ArgType::Text {
                min: x,
                max: y,
                format: g,
            },
        ) => f == g && x <= a && b <= y,
        (ArgType::Enum { variants: a }, ArgType::Enum { variants: b }) => {
            a.iter().all(|v| b.contains(v))
        }
        (
            ArgType::List { .. } | ArgType::ListRange { .. },
            ArgType::List { .. } | ArgType::ListRange { .. },
        ) => {
            let parts = |kind: &ArgType| match kind {
                ArgType::List { of, max } => (0, *max, of.clone()),
                ArgType::ListRange { of, min, max } => (*min, *max, of.clone()),
                _ => unreachable!(),
            };
            let (a, b, element) = parts(old);
            let (x, y, next) = parts(new);
            x <= a
                && b <= y
                && match (element, next) {
                    (None, None) => true,
                    (Some(a), Some(b)) => accepts(&a, &b),
                    _ => false,
                }
        }
        (ArgType::IntRange { .. }, ArgType::Int) => true,
        (ArgType::IntRange { min: a, max: b }, ArgType::IntRange { min: x, max: y }) => {
            x <= a && b <= y
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}
fn word(name: &str) -> bool {
    !name.is_empty() && name.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_')
}
fn offset(text: &str, line: u32, col: u32) -> usize {
    let start = text
        .split_inclusive('\n')
        .take(line.saturating_sub(1) as usize)
        .map(str::len)
        .sum::<usize>();

        .sum::<usize>();
    start
        + text[start..]
            .chars()
            .take(col.saturating_sub(1) as usize)
            .map(char::len_utf8)
            .sum::<usize>()
}
fn declaration(text: &str, line: u32) -> (usize, usize) {
    let start = offset(text, line, 1);
    let end = text
        .split_inclusive('\n')
        .enumerate()
        .skip(line as usize)
        .find(|(_, s)| {
            !s.trim().is_empty() && !s.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) && !s.starts_with("--")
        })
        .map(|(i, _)| offset(text, i as u32 + 1, 1))
        .unwrap_or(text.len());
    (start, end)
}
fn remap_type(kind: &mut ast::ScalarType, names: &BTreeMap<String, String>) {
    match &mut kind.kind {
        TypeKind::Ref { table } => {
            if let Some(name) = names.get(table) {
                *table = name.clone()
            }
        }
        TypeKind::List { item, .. } => remap_type(item, names),
        _ => (),
    }
}
// Closed, capture-avoiding alpha conversion of a single source module. Complex
// declaration families require an explicitly authored compatible source.
struct MappingError {reason:String,unsupported:bool}
impl From<String> for MappingError {
    fn from(reason:String)->Self{Self{reason,unsupported:false}}
}
impl From<&str> for MappingError {
    fn from(reason:&str)->Self{reason.to_owned().into()}
}
fn alpha(target: &[Source], current: &[Source]) -> Result<Option<Vec<Source>>, MappingError> {
    if target.len() != 1 || current.len() != 1 {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let old = parser::parse_document(&target[0].0, &target[0].1).0;
    let (file, text) = &current[0];
    let doc = parser::parse_document(file, text).0;
    if old.tables.iter().chain(&doc.tables).any(|t| !word(&t.name)) {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let mut names = BTreeMap::new();
    for next in &doc.tables {
        if old.tables.iter().any(|t| t.name == next.name) {
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(prior) = old.tables.iter().find(|t| {
            next.renamed_from.as_ref() == Some(&t.name)
                || t.renamed_from.as_ref() == Some(&next.name)
        }) {
            names.insert(next.name.clone(), prior.name.clone());
        }
    }
    if names.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    // A legal table name may also spell a primitive type. Reprinting that
    // identity in an argument/column must never turn a nominal Ref into Int,
    // Principal, etc. Validate the parser's actual type interpretation.
    for name in names.values() {
        let probe = format!("table __rollback_type_probe:\n  value: {name}\n");
        let (parsed, diagnostics) = parser::parse_document("rollback-type-probe.q", &probe);
        if !diagnostics.is_empty()
            || !matches!(parsed.tables.first().and_then(|t|t.columns.first()).map(|c|&c.type_.kind),Some(TypeKind::Ref{table}) if table==name)
        {
            return Err(
                "a table identity spelling reparses as a scalar or unsupported reference type"
                    .into(),
            );
        }
    }
    let outputs = doc
        .tables
        .iter()
        .map(|t| names.get(&t.name).unwrap_or(&t.name))
        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    if outputs.len() != doc.tables.len() || names.keys().any(|name| outputs.contains(name)) {
        return Err(
            "the declared table mapping is ambiguous or captures a current table name".into(),
        );
    }
    let reserved = names
        .keys()
        .chain(names.values())
        .cloned()
        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut capture = doc.shapes.iter().any(|s| reserved.contains(&s.name));
    capture |= doc.tables.iter().any(|t| {
        t.rules
            .iter()
            .chain(t.column_reads.iter().map(|(_, r)| r))
            .any(|r| r.bindings.iter().any(|n| reserved.contains(n)))
    });
    fn bindings(body: &[ast::Stmt], reserved: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
        body.iter().any(|s| match s {
            ast::Stmt::Bind { names, .. } => names.iter().any(|n| reserved.contains(n)),
            ast::Stmt::For { name, body, .. } => {
                reserved.contains(name) || bindings(body, reserved)
            }
            ast::Stmt::If {
                then_body,
                else_body,
                ..
            } => bindings(then_body, reserved) || bindings(else_body, reserved),
            ast::Stmt::As { body, .. } => bindings(body, reserved),
            _ => false,
        })
    }
    capture |= doc
        .programs
        .iter()
        .any(|p| bindings(&p.prelude, &reserved) || bindings(&p.body, &reserved));
    capture |= doc
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|f| matches!(&f.body,ast::FunctionBody::Block(body) if bindings(body,&reserved)));
    for args in doc
        .programs
        .iter()
        .map(|p| &p.args)
        .chain(doc.functions.iter().map(|p| &p.args))
        .chain(doc.actions.iter().map(|p| &p.args))
    {
        capture |= args.iter().any(|a| reserved.contains(&a.name));
    }
    let mut inspect = doc.clone();
    let mut unsupported = false;
    let mut check = |e: &mut ast::Expr| match e {
        ast::Expr::Name { name, .. } => capture |= reserved.contains(name),
        ast::Expr::Walk { .. } | ast::Expr::Merge(_) => unsupported = true,
        _ => (),
    };
    crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut inspect, &mut check);
    for inv in &mut inspect.invariants {
        crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut inv.value, &mut check);
    }
    for table in &mut inspect.tables {
        for rule in table
            .rules
            .iter_mut()
            .chain(table.column_reads.iter_mut().map(|(_, r)| r))
        {
            crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut rule.value, &mut check);
        }
        for column in &mut table.columns {
            for value in [&mut column.read, &mut column.default]
                .into_iter()
                .flatten()
            {
                crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value, &mut check);
            }
        }
        for (value, _) in &mut table.final_exists {
            crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value, &mut check);
        }
    }
    if unsupported {
        return Err(
            "a recursive or merge expression needs an authored compatible table mapping".into(),
        );
    }
    if capture {
        return Err("a table identity mapping captures a local, argument or shape name".into());
    }
    if !doc.actions.is_empty()
        || !doc.effects.is_empty()
        || !doc.maintains.is_empty()
        || !doc.timelines.is_empty()
        || !doc.views.is_empty()
        || !doc.feed_views.is_empty()
        || !doc.backfills.is_empty()
        || !doc.downfills.is_empty()
        || !doc.extractions.is_empty()
        || doc.programs.iter().any(|p| p.delivery.is_some())
        || doc
            .tables
            .iter()
            .any(|t| t.receipts.is_some() || t.columns.iter().any(|c| c.maintained.is_some()))
    {
        return Err(MappingError{reason:"this table identity mapping includes a derived, receipt or migration declaration outside the closed current-source adapter".into(),unsupported:true});
    }
    let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(file, text);
    let mut edits = Vec::<(usize, usize, String)>::new();
    for table in &doc.tables {
        let mut source = super::release_bridge::retained_declaration(file, text, table, &names)?;
        if let Some(name) = names.get(&table.name) {
            let prior = old
                .tables
                .iter()
                .find(|t| &t.name == name)
                .ok_or("missing predecessor identity")?;
            source = header(
                file,
                &source,
                &table.name,
                name,
                prior.renamed_from.as_deref(),
            )?;
        }
        let (a, b) = declaration(text, table.site.line);
        edits.push((a, b, source));
    }
    for inv in &doc.invariants {
        let table = names.get(&inv.table).unwrap_or(&inv.table);
        let mut value = inv.value.clone();
        crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut value, &mut |e| match e {
            ast::Expr::Read(r)
            | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }
            | ast::Expr::Created { read: r, .. } => {
                if let Some(name) = names.get(&r.table) {
                    r.table = name.clone()
                }
            }
            ast::Expr::Write { table, .. } => {
                if let Some(name) = names.get(table) {
                    *table = name.clone()
                }
            }
            _ => (),
        });
        let target = if let Some(column) = &inv.column {

pub fn loosen(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->Bridge {
    if !crate::compile(current).invariants.is_empty() {
        let mut allow_guarded=false;
        let complete=super::release_bridge_current::prepare(target,current,&mut allow_guarded);
        if complete.diagnostics.is_empty() || !allow_guarded{return complete;}
        // A complete current closure is preferred, but a selective guarded
        // predecessor is also truthful when its retained contracts compile.
        let guarded=super::release_bridge_current::guarded(target,current);
        if guarded.diagnostics.is_empty(){return guarded;}
        return Bridge{diagnostics:complete.diagnostics.into_iter().chain(guarded.diagnostics).collect(),..guarded};
    }
    let guarded=loosen_guarded(target,current);
    if !guarded.diagnostics.is_empty(){
        let complete=super::release_bridge_current::prepare(target,current,&mut false);
        if complete.diagnostics.is_empty(){return complete;}
        let current_bridge=super::release_bridge_current::guarded(target,current);
        if current_bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(){return current_bridge;}
    }
    guarded
}

                // schema. Never reintroduce predecessor-only values behind that

                        TypeKind::Ref{table}=>{if let Some((_,_,next))=tables.get(table){*table=next.name.clone();}},
                        TypeKind::List{item,..}=>normalize(item,tables),
                        TypeKind::Enum{variants}=>variants.sort(), _=>()
                    }
                }
                normalize(&mut current_domain,&new);normalize(&mut joined_domain,&new);
                if current_domain!=joined_domain {

                }

"""EV-1 public release witness, using the serving harness's owned cluster/children."""
# @ref LLP 3000.009.001.000#3.5 — configured deploy/serve and a store-independent wire comparison.
import json, time

V1 = '''use identity
table notes:
  n: int
  memo: json <=100 ?
  site: text <=200 format url ?
  by byN: n, id
  public
  insert <- allow
  update <- allow
  online only
table retired:
  text: text <=100
  public
  insert <- allow
  online only
mutation put(key: id, n: int, memo: json <=100 ?, site: text <=200 format url ?):
  upsert notes[key] {n, memo, site}
  return n
mutation archive(key: id, text: text <=100):
  insert retired {id: key, text}
  return text
query peek(key: id):
  return notes[key]
query archived(key: id):
  return retired[key]
query ordered():
  return notes first 10 by byN
'''
V2 = '''use identity
table notes:
  n: int
  extra: text <=100 ?
  count: int default 7
  by byCount: count, id
  public
  insert <- allow
  update <- allow
  online only
table added:
  n: int
  public
  insert <- allow
  online only
mutation put(key: id, n: int):
  upsert notes[key] {n}
  return n
mutation add(key: id, n: int):
  insert added {id: key, n}
  return n
query peek(key: id):
  return notes[key]
query addedRow(key: id):
  return added[key]
query ordered():
  return notes where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount
'''

V3 = V1.replace('  memo: json <=100 ?\n', '  memo: json <=100 ?\n  extra: text <=100 ?\n  count: int default 7\n')

def witness(h, pg_url):
    work, cli, start, stop, http = (h[k] for k in ('WORK','cli','start','stop','http'))
    transcripts = {}
    for backend, target in [('postgres', pg_url), ('sqlite', None)]:
        deployment = work / ('ev1-' + backend)
        records = []
        assert V3 != V1 and 'format url ?' in V3
        for version, source in [(1,V1),(2,V2),(3,V3)]:
            (work/'app/snapback/schema.q').write_text(source)
            args = ['deploy', str(deployment)]
            if version == 3: args += ['--restore-dormant']
            cli(args, url=target)
            child, base = start('ev1-'+backend+'-v'+str(version),target,deployment=deployment)
            guest = http(base,'POST','/auth/guest',{})['session']
            token = guest['token']
            state = http(base,'GET','/schema',token=token)
            assert state['generation']==version,state
            def capture(label, value):
                # Each backend mints its own guest principal. Check its value
                # before excluding that nondeterministic identity from comparison.
                if ' q ' in label: assert value['viewer']==guest['principal'],value
                if 'store_id' in value: assert value['store_id']==state['store_id'],value
                independent = {k:v for k,v in value.items() if k not in ('store_id','now','viewer')}
                records.append((label,json.dumps(independent,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'))))
                return value
            def mutate(name, identity, args):
                return capture(f'v{version} m {name}',http(base,'POST','/m/'+name,{'id':identity,'args':args,'store_id':state['store_id']},token))
            def query(name, args):
                return capture(f'v{version} q {name}',http(base,'POST','/q/'+name,{'args':args},token))
            if version == 1:
                assert mutate('put','ev1-a',{'key':'a','n':2,'memo':'雪\0exact','site':'https://example.com'})['state']=='sent'
                assert mutate('put','ev1-b',{'key':'b','n':1,'memo':None,'site':None})['state']=='sent'
                assert mutate('archive','ev1-z',{'key':'z','text':'retained row'})['state']=='sent'
                first = query('peek',{'key':'a'})['data']
                archived = query('archived',{'key':'z'})['data']
                assert [r['n'] for r in query('ordered',{})['data']]==[1,2]
            elif version == 2:
                changed = query('peek',{'key':'a'})['data']
                assert changed == {'id':'a','n':2,'extra':None,'count':7},changed
                assert len(query('ordered',{})['data'])==2
                assert mutate('add','ev1-new',{'key':'new','n':9})['state']=='sent'
                assert query('addedRow',{'key':'new'})['data']['n']==9
            else:
                assert query('peek',{'key':'a'})['data']==dict(first,extra=None,count=7)
                assert query('archived',{'key':'z'})['data']==archived
                assert [r['n'] for r in query('ordered',{})['data']]==[1,2]
            stop(child)
            if backend == 'postgres':
                deadline=time.monotonic()+15
                while True:
                    doctor=json.loads(cli(['doctor','--release',str(deployment),'--json'],url=target).stdout)
                    if doctor['store']['expired']: break
                    assert time.monotonic()<deadline,doctor
                    time.sleep(.1)
        transcripts[backend]=json.dumps(records,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':')).encode()
        (work/('ev1-'+backend+'-wire.json')).write_bytes(transcripts[backend])
        print('P1-EV-1',backend,'PASS: v1 writes, v2 additions/retirements/indexes, v3 exact restoration',flush=True)
    assert transcripts['postgres']==transcripts['sqlite'], 'EV-1 wire bytes differ'
    (work/'app/snapback/schema.q').write_text(h['source'])
    print('P1-EV-1 PASS: byte-identical wire after removing store_id, now and the checked minted guest viewer',flush=True)
    large_default_witness(h, pg_url.replace('schema=ev1', 'schema=ev1_large'))


def large_default_witness(h, pg_url):
    """A restored raw parent image must widen /changes identically on both stores."""
    work, cli, start, stop, http = (h[k] for k in ('WORK','cli','start','stop','http'))
    v1 = '''use identity
table notes:
  n: int
  memo: json <=100 ?
  public
  insert <- allow
  delete <- allow
  online only
table children:
  note: notes on delete restrict
  public
  insert <- allow
  online only
mutation put(key: id):
  insert notes {id: key, n: 1}
mutation erase(key: id):
  delete notes[key]
query peek(key: id):
  return notes[key]
'''
    sibling = "  payload: text <=307200 default '" + 'x' * (300 * 1024) + "'\n"
    versions = [v1, v1.replace('  memo: json <=100 ?\n', sibling),
        v1.replace('  memo: json <=100 ?\n', '  memo: json <=100 ?\n' + sibling)]
    answers = {}
    for backend, target in [('postgres', pg_url), ('sqlite', None)]:
        deployment = work / ('ev1-large-' + backend)
        for version, source in enumerate(versions, 1):
            (work/'app/snapback/schema.q').write_text(source)
            cli(['deploy', str(deployment)] + (['--restore-dormant'] if version == 3 else []), url=target)
            child, base = start(f'ev1-large-{backend}-v{version}', target, deployment=deployment)
            guest = http(base, 'POST', '/auth/guest', {})['session']
            token = guest['token']
            state = http(base, 'GET', '/schema', token=token)
            assert state['generation'] == version, state
            def mutate(name):
                return http(base, 'POST', '/m/'+name, {'id':f'large-{name}',
                    'args':{'key':'a'}, 'store_id':state['store_id']}, token)
            if version == 1:
                assert mutate('put')['state'] == 'sent'
            else:
                row = http(base, 'POST', '/q/peek', {'args':{'key':'a'}}, token)
                assert row['data']['payload'] == 'x' * (300 * 1024)
                if version == 3:
                    assert row['data']['memo'] is None
                    assert mutate('erase')['state'] == 'sent'
                    answer = http(base, 'GET', f"/changes?since={row['seq']}&wait=0", token=token)
                    answers[backend] = {k:v for k,v in answer.items() if k not in ('store_id','now')}
                    print('P1-EV-1 300 KiB', backend, '/changes:', answers[backend], flush=True)
            stop(child)
            if backend == 'postgres':
                deadline = time.monotonic() + 15
                while True:
                    doctor = json.loads(cli(['doctor','--release',str(deployment),'--json'],url=target).stdout)
                    if doctor['store']['expired']: break
                    assert time.monotonic() < deadline, doctor
                    time.sleep(.1)
    assert answers['sqlite']['changes'] is None, answers
    assert answers['postgres'] == answers['sqlite'], '300 KiB restored parent /changes differs'
    (work/'ev1-large-changes.json').write_text(json.dumps(answers, sort_keys=True))
    (work/'app/snapback/schema.q').write_text(h['source'])
    print('P1-EV-1 PASS: 300 KiB restored parent /changes is null on both stores', flush=True)
//! Current query dependencies must travel with a selective guarded bridge.
use snapback4_core::{Context, Engine, Value};
fn source(text: &str) -> Vec<(String, String)> { vec![("schema.q".into(), text.into())] }
fn rows(bridge: &crate::release_bridge::Bridge, values: &[(&str, i64, i64)], query: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    engine.seed(values.iter().map(|(id, n, rank)| ("notes".into(), Value::Row([
        ("id".into(), Value::Id((*id).into())), ("n".into(), Value::Int(*n)),
        ("rank".into(), Value::Int(*rank)), ("amount".into(), Value::Int(5)),
    ].into_iter().collect())))).unwrap();
    let result = engine.query(query, Context::new("alice", Default::default())).unwrap();
    let Value::Array(rows) = result.data else { panic!("expected rows") };
    rows.into_iter().map(|r| match r.field("id").unwrap() { Value::Id(id) => id.clone(), _ => panic!("id") }).collect()
}
#[test]
fn current_index_is_carried_with_its_endpoint() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  by byN: n, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byN\n";
    let current = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  count: int default 7\n  by byCount: count, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable added:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount\n";
    let bridge = crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old), &source(current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    assert!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes.contains_key("byN"));
    assert_eq!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes["byCount"].columns(), ["count", "id"]);
    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    engine.seed([("a", 2), ("b", 1)].map(|(id, n)| ("notes".into(), Value::Row([
        ("id".into(), Value::Id(id.into())), ("n".into(), Value::Int(n)),
    ].into_iter().collect())))).unwrap();
    let result = engine.query("ordered", Context::new("alice", Default::default())).unwrap();
    let Value::Array(rows) = result.data else { panic!("expected rows") };
    assert_eq!(rows.iter().map(|r| r.field("n").unwrap().clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), [Value::Int(2), Value::Int(1)]);
}
#[test]
fn same_named_changed_index_uses_current_context() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  rank: int\n  amount: int\n  by byOrder: n, id\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byOrder\n";
    let current = old.replace("amount: int", "amount: int ?").replace("byOrder: n", "byOrder: rank");
    let bridge = crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old), &source(&current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bridge.diagnostics);
    assert_eq!(rows(&bridge, &[("a", 1, 9), ("b", 2, 0)], "ordered"), ["b", "a"]);
}
#[test]
fn same_named_word_index_uses_current_field_selection() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  title: text <=40\n  body: text <=40\n  amount: int\n  by bySearch: words(title), id\n  public\n  online only\nquery find(q: text <=40):\n  return notes matching q first 10 by bySearch\n";
    let current = old.replace("amount: int", "amount: int ?").replace("words(title)", "words(body)");
    let bridge = crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old), &source(&current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    assert_eq!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes["bySearch"].word_component().unwrap().1, ["body"]);
}

#[test]
fn equivalent_unique_key_name_is_not_a_current_index_alias() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  unique byOwner: owner\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byOwner\n";
    let current = "table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  count: int default 7\n  unique byCurrentOwner: owner\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byCurrentOwner\n";
    let bridge = crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old), &source(current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    assert!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes["byCurrentOwner"].unique);
}
#[test]
fn declared_plain_column_identity_is_not_an_index_meaning_change() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  by byOwner: owner, id\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byOwner\n";
    let current = old.replace("owner: principal", "customer: principal renamed from owner").replace("byOwner: owner", "byOwner: customer");
    let before = crate::compile(&source(old));
    let after = crate::compile(&source(&current));
    assert!(before.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", before.diagnostics);
    assert!(after.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", after.diagnostics);
    let old_doc = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", old).0;
    let new_doc = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", &current).0;
    assert!(!crate::release_bridge_indexes::current_context_needed(&[old_doc], &[new_doc], &before.schema, &after.schema, &Default::default()));
}

#[test]
fn new_plain_index_uses_declared_predecessor_column_identity() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  owner: principal\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  by byN: n, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byN\n";
    let current = "table notes:\n  n: int ?\n  customer: principal renamed from owner\n  count: int default 7\n  by byCount: count, customer, id\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount\n";
    let bridge = crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old), &source(current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    assert_eq!(bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes["byCount"].columns(), ["count", "owner", "id"]);
}

#[test]
fn plain_index_transport_keeps_index_label_and_business_literals() {
    let old = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", "table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  online only\n").0;
    let raw = "  unique customer: customer else TAKEN 'customer stays customer'";
    let text = format!("table notes:\n  customer: principal renamed from owner\n{raw}\n  online only\n");
    let current = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", &text).0;
    let next = &current.tables[0];
    let result = crate::release_bridge_indexes::plain_declaration("schema.q", raw, &next.indexes[0], &old.tables[0], next);
    assert_eq!(result, "  unique customer: owner else TAKEN 'customer stays customer'");
}
#[test]
fn plain_column_transport_never_collapses_independent_current_fields() {
    let old = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", "table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  online only\n").0;
    let current = crate::parser::parse_document("schema.q", "table notes:\n  customer: principal renamed from owner\n  owner: principal\n  online only\n").0;
    assert!(crate::release_bridge_indexes::column_names(&old.tables[0], &current.tables[0]).is_none());
}

#[test]
fn current_unique_index_replaces_same_named_predecessor_nonunique_index() {
    let old = "table notes:\n  n: int\n  code: text <=20\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  by byN: n, id\n  by byCustomer: code\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes first 10 by byN\n";
    let current = "table notes:\n  n: int ?\n  label: text <=20 renamed from code\n  count: int default 7\n  by byCount: count, label, id\n  unique byCustomer: label\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return notes where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount\n";
    let bridge = crate::release_bridge::loosen(&source(old), &source(current));
    assert!(bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bridge.diagnostics);
    let bound = crate::compile(&bridge.sources);
    let index = &bound.schema.tables["notes"].indexes["byCustomer"];
    assert!(index.unique);
    assert_eq!(index.columns(), ["code"]);
}

#### Current independent intent and authored execution closure — Integration 23

A generated view must not restore a predecessor workflow that violates current
independent intent. Copying invariant declarations, table rules or `require`
statements alone is insufficient: a changed literal, helper, grant mutation,
write key, default, control path, returned value or system/time dependency can
change the reachable effect without changing any copied guard. This applies
both to newly declared intent and intent already present in the predecessor.
Finite challenge campaigns are regression evidence, not an equivalence proof.

The presence of an invariant never makes ordinary schema evolution require a
view. The wave-25 replay exposed the Integration 23 regression in the earlier
"any invariant plus changed code" rule: it falsely refused ten of fifty v2
deploys, including simple renames and a split with a typed down. View registration
uses structural widening and old-row foresight facts, including hiding and
misrepresentation, rather than a general executable-digest change.

Final-existence obligations compare compiled constraints under injective declared
table and column identities, including typed reference keys and ordered plain-index
columns. Index labels and source spelling are not semantic changes; missing or
changed obligations still trigger registration. Literal values remain literal.

There is one repair protection independent of row-domain widening: behind
unchanged authored data/intent declarations and executable signatures, a changed
implementation with declared intent prepares a view. Signatures include module
identity and operation wire contracts; function/program bodies are excluded.
Thus a literal/helper-only policy repair or an executable-only worker/native
repair cannot restore the older executable behind unchanged intent. Identical
executable content remains a no-op; ordinary data/intent/contract evolution is
subject to the visibility trigger instead. Compiler-owned timeline event slots
are delivery metadata, not authored predecessor cells; retiring a rebuildable
feed alone does not require a rollback view. Ordinary authored fields in those
same source tables remain subject to the complete visibility check.

Preparation first attempts the **complete current authored source closure**,
including every declaration, schema domain, default, helper, program, job and
invariant. That strategy requires every predecessor table/column spelling and
operation name/kind and accepts every old argument under its wire name (including
exported aliases). An explicitly authored parameter alias may retain an old wire
name after a canonical rename. Every old spelling for one position must resolve
to the same current position, and distinct old positions must remain distinct.
Current result semantics remain a disclosed RESULT change. The same complete
closure is also a fallback for a guard/helper transport failure without intent.

If the strict complete-closure contract is unavailable, an intent-bearing view
uses a **guarded current workflow**. Its schema, defaults, helpers, programs,
shapes, jobs, aliases and independent intent remain the current authored source;
it never copies an old executable body or helper. A removed nullable,
default-free, non-engine, non-maintained predecessor field may remain absent in
this view instead of fabricating its value. The view reports that result change;
the old cell stays retained, and an old UI may need its usual missing-field
fallback. Required/defaulted predecessor fields and every predecessor table
still need a truthful compatible representation. A declared scalar or asset/reference
column identity rename may use its current spelling and current result semantics,
with an explicit old-UI result-change disclosure; this uses the compiler
validated identity mapping rather than inventing a predecessor value. The
same closed, capture-checked table alpha adapter may run before this
guarded-current strategy, so a nullable field gap does not discard a valid
declared table identity. Its current authority is compared under those identities
before strategy selection; an unsafe or unsupported alpha mapping still refuses.
No legacy schema field is introduced into current expression/name resolution.

Every predecessor query and every retained export must preserve its old accepted
wire inputs, aliases and distinct input positions. An unused parameter-alias slot
may retain an old spelling on the same current canonical argument; collisions,
rebinding, narrowing and unsupported mappings refuse. A missing old exported
alias may point only at an already authored compatible current endpoint.
Current explicit/rebound aliases retain their exact current target and guards.
A genuinely removed mutation, job or action may instead pause: its absence and
`E_OP` are named, and the author can supply a compatible current guarded endpoint
if that old workflow is still needed. Old code is never revived to make a removed
write available. Queries remain required so a view does not silently discard the
predecessor read surface. Writer/CAS, continuation and other activation gates
continue to apply to both strategies.

Without independent intent, the original selective guarded bridge remains
available. Its dependency checks resolve qualified helper calls to the same
validated global helper identity as the compiler, including transitive calls. Helper parameter types and row-reference AST slots
are compared under forward/inverse declared table identities; business strings,
member spellings and helper identities are never token-replaced.
A replaced or rebound exported alias or untransportable guard/dependency falls back to the
current workflow strategies above. Mixed-domain fallback uses the same compiled
qualified endpoint/alias contract; there is no unqualified source-text shortcut.
The selective predecessor workflow keeps its own accepted old wire contract;
current aliases cannot merge or rebind distinct old input positions behind
transported current guards. The complete-current and guarded-current strategies
above instead require the compiled current endpoint to accept every old input.
Guarded exports retain complete statement
order, query filters, helper calls and declared row identities; a differing ordered body or named-shape dependency
selects the current workflow instead of reconstructing authorization from the
last textual binding alone. Execution comparisons retain the complete AST,
including every merge lane prefix, source, loop domain, filter and projection;
only diagnostic source locations and validated row-identity spellings are
normalized. Human-readable expression printing is not a semantic signature.
Unrelated schema/domain additions are not guard
dependencies, and declared table identities are compared under their typed
renames rather than by raw spelling. Named shape definitions (including transitive
spreads) belong to that execution context; unchanged shape-use text does not
establish unchanged guard meaning. Field-authority mismatches participate in
strategy selection before the CLI admission gate, so a truthful guarded-current
view can be selected instead of refusing a valid deployment. Current shared-column defaults and authority are
checked after compilation.
A failed/capturing/ambiguous typed identity mapping cannot authorize fallback.
Source edits use original UTF-8 spans bounded by original file length even
without a final LF. Quota relaxation is structural registration input; tightening
on the same grouping is not. General constraint advice is not a second, less
precise registration predicate. Refusals identify the compatible guarded endpoint,
wire alias or truthful reverse projection to author. A hiding acknowledgment
never authorizes restoring an unsafe workflow, and ordinary security findings,
gates and runtime invariant boundaries still apply.

For this complete-current-closure path only, input-domain inclusion additionally
admits same-format text intervals whose current minimum is no larger and maximum
no smaller than the predecessor's. Identical domains, optional widening, enum
supersets and integer interval widening retain their existing meaning. New optional arguments may be omitted by old callers and take the current
language null value or their explicit authored default. Bounded lists admit recursive element-domain inclusion
only when current cardinality bounds contain the complete predecessor interval
and both element domains are declared (or both absent). Other formats, nominal
kinds and unsupported domains refuse.
This explicitly extends the initial closed rule above; it neither converts old
arguments nor broadens the separate selective guard/endpoint transport matcher.

The sole automatic spelling adapter is capture-avoiding alpha conversion of
explicit table identities in one source module. It rewrites typed table slots,
reference types and invariant subjects, retaining current bodies and literal
business strings. Every destination identity must reparse as a nominal reference
type, never a primitive scalar; argument aliases/defaults/deprecation metadata
remain outside replaced type spans. Mappings must be injective and cannot capture table, argument,
local-binding or shape names. Derived declarations, migrations, deliveries,
actions/effects, recursive/merge expressions and unsupported source forms require
an authored compatible source. The adapted closure recompiles and must satisfy
the same complete predecessor contract checks. Multi-module sources may use the
unchanged current closure directly; automatic cross-module renaming is not
claimed. No runtime policy, default, selection order or value conversion is
synthesized. Native/worker bundle requirements remain in force. Both predecessor and current
stage automatic/named worker dependencies are checked; a newly introduced
current worker cannot disappear from a generated view. The CLI additionally
compares complete executable digests, excluding registration/compiler/location
metadata: changing only worker/native bytes behind unchanged Quarry intent
triggers preparation and the same bundle boundary. Identical executable content
remains a no-op. Until complete immutable
bundles are captured and qualified, those generated views refuse.

For a pre-0.4.39 deployment without registered views, default rollback either
succeeds under writer-enforced visibility or refuses atomically with the logical
target and both recovery steps: `rollback <deployment> --to N --exact` (preview
its hiding first) and "redeploy with 0.4.39 to register a view". An equal-source
redeploy with an obsolete/missing view set still performs registration rather
than returning an unqualified UNCHANGED receipt.

fn header(
    file: &str,
    source: &str,
    from: &str,
    to: &str,
    prior: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    if prior.is_some_and(|p| !word(p)) {
        return Err("qualified predecessor table origin needs an authored source".into());
    }
    let line = source.lines().next().ok_or("missing table header")?;
    let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(file, line);
    let name = tokens
        .get(1)
        .filter(|t| t.kind == Kind::Word && t.value == from)
        .ok_or("missing table name span")?;
    let mut edits = vec![(
        offset(line, name.site.line, name.site.col),
        offset(line, name.site.line, name.site.col) + from.len(),
        to.to_owned(),
    )];
    if let Some(at) = tokens.iter().enumerate().skip(2).find_map(|(i,t)| (t.value=="renamed").then_some(i)) {
        let last = tokens
            .get(at + 2)
            .filter(|_| tokens.get(at + 1).is_some_and(|t| t.value == "from"))
            .ok_or("invalid table origin span")?;
        edits.push((
            offset(line, tokens[at].site.line, tokens[at].site.col),
            offset(line, last.site.line, last.site.col) + last.value.len(),
            prior
                .map(|p| format!("renamed from {p}"))
                .unwrap_or_default(),
        ));
    } else if let Some(prior) = prior {
        let colon = tokens
            .iter()
            .find(|t| t.value == ":")
            .ok_or("missing table header colon")?;
        let a = offset(line, colon.site.line, colon.site.col);
        edits.push((a, a, format!(" renamed from {prior}")));
    }
    let mut out = source.to_owned();
    edits.sort_by_key(|(a, _, _)| std::cmp::Reverse(*a));
    for (a, b, value) in edits {
        out.replace_range(a..b, &value);
    }
    Ok(out)
}
pub(super) fn arg_type(
    text: &str,
    tokens: &[lexer::Token],
    arg: &ast::ArgDecl,
) -> Result<(usize, usize), String> {
    let at = tokens
        .iter()
        .position(|t| t.site == arg.site)
        .ok_or("missing argument source span")?;
    let colon = (at..tokens.len())
        .find(|i| tokens[*i].value == ":")
        .ok_or("missing argument type colon")?;
    let start = tokens.get(colon + 1).ok_or("missing argument type")?;
    let mut depth = 0_i32;
    for (ordinal, t) in tokens.iter().skip(colon + 1).enumerate() {
        // `alias` and `default` are legal first nominal type tokens too.
        if ordinal > 0
            && depth == 0
            && (matches!(
                t.value.as_str(),
                "," | ")" | "=" | "default" | "alias" | "renamed" | "deprecated"
            ) || t.kind == Kind::Newline)
        {
            return Ok((
                offset(text, start.site.line, start.site.col),
                text[..offset(text, t.site.line, t.site.col)]
                    .trim_end()
                    .len(),
            ));
        }
        match t.value.as_str() {
            "(" | "[" | "<" => depth += 1,
            ")" | "]" | ">" => depth -= 1,
            _ => (),
        }
    }
    Err("unterminated argument type source span".into())
}

pub struct Index {
    pub components: Vec<IndexComponent>,
    pub unique: bool,
}

impl Index {
    pub fn of(columns: &[&str], unique: bool) -> Self {
        Self { components: columns.iter().map(|c| IndexComponent::Column((*c).to_owned())).collect(), unique }
    }

    /// Ordered key names: columns and time-function names. A `words(..)`
    /// component contributes nothing here; `word_component` names it.
    /// Use `IndexComponent::column` for actual row fields (such as rule pins).
    pub fn columns(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        self.components.iter().filter_map(|c| c.key_name().cloned()).collect()
    }

    pub fn is_plain(&self) -> bool {
        self.components.iter().all(|c| matches!(c, IndexComponent::Column(_)))
    }

    pub fn uses_column(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
        self.components.iter().any(|component| match component {
            IndexComponent::Column(column) => column == name,
            IndexComponent::Calendar(key) => key.column == name,
            IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields, .. } => fields.iter().any(|field| field == name),
            IndexComponent::Contribution(maintain) => maintain.dependencies().contains(name),
        })
    }

    /// The `words(..)` component, if any: its position among the
    /// components and the text columns it tokenizes.
    pub fn word_component(&self) -> Option<(usize, &[String])> {
        self.components.iter().enumerate().find_map(|(position, component)| match component {
            IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields, .. } => Some((position, fields.as_slice())),
            IndexComponent::Column(_) | IndexComponent::Calendar(_) | IndexComponent::Contribution(_) => None,
        })
    }

    /// The columns after the first `components` components: a cursor's
    /// suffix, and the order a page continues in. Counted in components,
    /// so a scan whose prefix ends with a word skips the word too.
    pub fn suffix_after(&self, components: usize) -> Vec<String> {
        self.components.iter().skip(components).filter_map(|c| c.key_name().cloned()).collect()
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum IndexComponent {
    /// Internal selected physical source rows; an optional following expiry component
    /// orders the group by deadline (null last), then the appended source id.
    Contribution(Box<Maintain>),
    Column(String),
    Calendar(crate::maintain::CalendarKey),
    WordPrefixes { fields: Vec<String>, #[serde(default)] contract: serde_json::Value },
}

impl IndexComponent {
    pub fn key_name(&self) -> Option<&String> {
        match self { Self::Calendar(key) => Some(&key.function), _ => self.column() }
    }
    pub fn key_type(&self, table: &Table) -> Option<ColumnType> {
        match self { Self::Calendar(_) => Some(ColumnType::Int), Self::Column(name) => table.columns.get(name).cloned(), _ => None }
    }
    pub fn value(&self, row: &Value) -> Option<Value> {
        match self { Self::Calendar(key) => Some(key.value(row)), Self::Column(name) => Some(row.field(name).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null)), _ => None }
    }
    pub fn column(&self) -> Option<&String> {
        match self {
            Self::Column(column) => Some(column),
            Self::Calendar(_) | Self::WordPrefixes { .. } | Self::Contribution(_) => None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Rules {
    pub read: RuleExpr,
    pub insert: RuleExpr,
    pub update: RuleExpr,
    pub delete: RuleExpr,
}

impl Rules {
    pub fn deny() -> Self {
        Self { read: RuleExpr::Deny, insert: RuleExpr::Deny, update: RuleExpr::Deny, delete: RuleExpr::Deny }
    }

    pub fn for_effect(&self, effect: Effect) -> &RuleExpr {
        match effect {
            Effect::Read => &self.read,
            Effect::Insert => &self.insert,
            Effect::Update => &self.update,
            Effect::Delete => &self.delete,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Effect {
    Read,
    Insert,
    Update,
    Delete,
}

impl fmt::Display for Effect {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(match self {
            Self::Read => "read",
            Self::Insert => "insert",
            Self::Update => "update",
            Self::Delete => "delete",
        })
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum RuleExpr {
    Public { reason: String },
    Allow,
    Deny,
    Eq(RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
    Ne(RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
    /// Ordered comparisons are confined to named update transitions and to
    /// insert rules over the row's own required int/time columns.
    Compare(crate::ir::BinaryOp, RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
    And(Vec<RuleExpr>),
    Or(Vec<RuleExpr>),
    Not(Box<RuleExpr>),
    Exists {
        table: String, index: String, key: Vec<RuleTerm>, provenance: KeyProvenance,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
        equalities: Vec<(String, RuleTerm)>,
    },
    /// True only when the indexed target row was inserted earlier in this
    /// same transaction; insert rules only.
    Created {
        table: String, index: String, key: Vec<RuleTerm>, provenance: KeyProvenance,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
        equalities: Vec<(String, RuleTerm)>,
    },
}

impl RuleExpr {
    pub fn is_public(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Public { .. })
    }

    pub fn is_uniformly_readable(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Public { .. } | Self::Allow)
    }
