    fn parse_index(&mut self, unique: bool) -> Fallible<IndexDecl> {
        let site = self.take(if unique { "unique" } else { "by" })?.site;
        let mut explicit = None;
        if self.at_kind(Kind::Word) && self.peek_at(1).value == ":" {
            explicit = Some(self.word()?.value);
            self.take(":")?;
        }
        let mut columns = Vec::new();
        let mut words = None;
        let mut computed = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
        loop {
            let component = self.word()?;
            if self.at(".") {
                return Err(syntax_with(&component.site, "table index columns cannot be reference paths", "index a column of this table; use recorded paths only on a maintain").guide("indexes"));
            }
            if component.value == "words" && self.at("(") {
                // `words(body, caption)`: one entry per distinct word of the
                // named text columns, in this position.
                if words.is_some() {
                    return Err(syntax_with(&component.site, "an index has one words(..) component", "words(body)"));
                }
                self.take("(")?;
                let mut fields = vec![self.word()?.value];
                while self.maybe(",") {
                    fields.push(self.word()?.value);
                }
                self.take(")")?;
                words = Some((columns.len(), fields));
            } else if matches!(component.value.as_str(), "day" | "hour" | "bucket" | "month" | "year") && self.at("(") {
                self.position -= 1;
                let expression = self.expression(0, &[",", "else", "<newline>"]).map_err(|error| {
                    if error.code == "E_QUARRY_MAINTAIN" { crate::diag::type_error(&error.site, error.message, "day(at), hour(at, 30m), or bucket(at, 2h, 30m); declare at: time on this table").guide("indexes") }
                    else { error }
                })?;
                if computed.insert(component.value.clone(), expression).is_some() {
                    return Err(syntax_with(&component.site, format!("index repeats {}(..); each time function occurs at most once per index", component.value), "keep one day, hour and bucket component per index; declare another index for another period").guide("indexes"));
                }
                columns.push(component.value);
            } else {
                columns.push(component.value);
            }
            if !self.maybe(",") {
                break;
            }
        }
        let explicit = explicit.or_else(|| unique.then(|| {
            let descriptor = (&columns, &words, computed.iter().map(|(name, value)| (name, crate::print::expression(value))).collect::<Vec<_>>(), true);
            let hash = snapback4_core::value::digest(&descriptor);
            format!("$intent:by{}:{}", columns.iter().map(|c| title(c)).collect::<String>(), &hash[..16])
        }));
        let Some(name) = explicit else {
            let inferred = format!("by{}", columns.iter().map(|c| title(c)).collect::<String>());
            return Err(syntax_with(&site, "index declarations require an explicit name", format!("{} {inferred}: {}", if unique { "unique" } else { "by" }, columns.join(", "))));
        };
        if let Some((position, fields)) = &words {
            if unique {
                return Err(syntax_with(&site, "a words(..) index cannot be unique: a row has one entry per word", format!("by {name}: {}", columns.iter().take(*position).cloned().chain(std::iter::once(format!("words({})", fields.join(", ")))).chain(columns.iter().skip(*position).cloned()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
            }
            if *position == columns.len() {
                return Err(syntax_with(&site, "words(..) is followed by the columns a search page is ordered by, ending in id", format!("by {name}: {}words({}), at, id", columns.iter().map(|c| format!("{c}, ")).collect::<String>(), fields.join(", "))));
            }
        }
        let refusal = if unique && self.maybe("else") {
            let code = self.word()?.value;
            let message = if self.at_kind(Kind::Str) { Some(self.take_kind(Kind::Str)?.value) } else { None };
            Some((code, message))
        } else { None };
        self.newline()?;
        Ok(IndexDecl { name, columns, unique, refusal, words, computed, site })
    }

        let site = keyword.site.clone();
        let name = self.binding_name()?;

//! Shared type-directed positional point selection for programs and rules.
// @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — identity wins; otherwise ordered compatible declared keys only.
use super::catalog::{Catalog, TableInfo};
use crate::{ast::{IndexDecl, ReadExpr}, diag::Diagnostic, types::Ty};

pub(super) fn candidates<'a>(info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, types: &[(Ty, bool)], synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>) -> Vec<&'a IndexDecl> {
    let identity = read.index.is_none() && types.len() == 1 &&
        (types[0].0.required() == Ty::Id || types[0].0.required() == Ty::Ref(read.table.clone())
            || (!types[0].0.is_optional() && matches!(types[0].0.required(),Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==read.table)));
    info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| !synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())))
        .filter(|index| match &read.index {
            Some(name) => &index.name == name,
            None if identity => index.name == "byId",
            None => index.unique && index.columns.len() == types.len(),
        })
        // Explicit indexes keep their existing mismatch diagnostics and range semantics.
        .filter(|index| read.index.is_some() || types.iter().zip(&index.columns).all(|((ty, literal), column)| {
            compatible(ty, *literal, &column_ty(info, index, column))
        })).collect()
}

fn column_ty(info: &TableInfo, index: &IndexDecl, column: &str) -> Ty {
    if index.computed.contains_key(column) { Ty::Int }
    else if column == "id" { Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone()) }
    else { info.columns.get(column).map(Ty::from_scalar).unwrap_or(Ty::Unknown) }
}

fn compatible(ty: &Ty, literal: bool, expected: &Ty) -> bool {
    ty.comparable(expected)
        || (!ty.is_optional() && matches!((ty.required(), expected.required()), (Ty::Row(a) | Ty::WriteRow(a), Ty::Ref(b)) if a == b))
        || (literal && expected.is_identity())
}

pub(super) fn refusal(
    info: &TableInfo,
    read: &ReadExpr,
    types: &[(Ty, bool)],
    candidates: &[&IndexDecl],
    catalog: &Catalog,
) -> Diagnostic {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — lead a wrong-table positional lookup with the foreign reference's actual table.
    if candidates.is_empty() && let Some((position, source)) = types.iter().enumerate().find_map(|(position, (ty, literal))| {
        match ty.required() {
            Ty::Ref(source) | Ty::Row(source) | Ty::WriteRow(source) if source != read.table
                && !info.indexes.iter()
                    .filter(|index| !catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())))
                    .filter(|index| read.index.as_ref().is_some_and(|name| name == &index.name)
                        || (read.index.is_none() && index.unique && index.columns.len() == types.len()))
                    .any(|index| index.columns.get(position)
                        .is_some_and(|column| compatible(ty, *literal, &column_ty(info, index, column)))) => Some((position, source)),
            _ => None,
        }
    }) {
        let value = read.keys.get(position).map(|key| crate::print::expression(&key.value)).unwrap_or_else(|| "this value".into());
        let mut expected = info.indexes.iter()
            .filter(|index| !catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())))
            .filter(|index| read.index.as_ref().is_some_and(|name| name == &index.name)
                || (read.index.is_none() && index.unique && index.columns.len() == types.len()))
            .filter_map(|index| index.columns.get(position).map(|column| column_ty(info, index, column).name()))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        expected.sort();
        expected.dedup();
        let expected = if expected.is_empty() { "a value accepted by a declared key".into() } else { expected.join(" or ") };
        return Diagnostic::new(
            &read.site,
            "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN",
            format!("`{value}` is a {source} reference, but {} key position {} expects {expected}; a reference from another table cannot select it", read.table, position + 1),
            format!("replace `{value}` with {expected}, and name the intended key columns or choose its index with `by`; if this is a population read, use `{} where .<column> = <value> first 20 by .id`", read.table),
        )
        .guide("reads");
    }
    let alternatives = if candidates.is_empty() && read.index.is_some() {
        info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| !catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>()
    } else { candidates.to_vec() };
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.7 — `budgets['general']` (lane 020): a text value names the declared
    // text key far more often than a row id, so that named form leads (and is --fix's choice).
    let mut alternatives = alternatives;
    alternatives.sort_by_key(|index| index.columns.iter().any(|column| column == "id"));
    let mut changes = Vec::new();
    for index in &alternatives {
        // Same-column opposite-order indexes need the explicit name as well.
        let duplicate = info.indexes.iter().any(|other| other.name != index.name && other.unique
            && other.columns.len() == index.columns.len() && other.columns.iter().all(|c| index.columns.contains(c)));
        if read.index.is_none() { changes.push(crate::repair::ReadChange::NamedKey { columns: index.columns.clone(), index: duplicate.then(|| index.name.clone()) }); }
        // Unknown explicit indexes and computed keys retain verified legacy alternatives.
        changes.push(crate::repair::ReadChange::Index(index.name.clone()));
    }
    let message = if alternatives.is_empty() {
        format!("{} has no declared unique key accepting these positional argument types in order; name the key columns, or use where for a population", read.table)
    } else {
        format!("{} positional argument types leave {} candidate keys; name the intended key columns or choose a compatible unique index explicitly", read.table, candidates.len())
    };
    let diagnostic = Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN", message, "").guide("reads").repair_read(&read.site, changes);
    // The user explicitly opts into one context-verified choice with --fix.
    if read.index.is_none() && candidates.len() > 1 { diagnostic.mechanical() } else { diagnostic }
}

//! ROLL at the shipped CLI boundary; private copies are removed on every exit.
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::{
    fs,
    path::PathBuf,
    process::{Command, Output},
    sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering},
};
const BIN: &str = env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_snapback4");

#[test]
fn current_first_worker_dependency_cannot_disappear_from_an_intent_view() {
    let app=App::new();
    let old="table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  amount: int 0..10\n  public\n  online only\nmutation create():\n  return insert docs {owner:viewer,amount:1}\n";
    app.source(old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.source(&format!("{}invariant docs.read only true\n",old.replace("amount: int 0..10","amount: int 0..20")));
    fs::write(app.0.join("snapback/worker.mjs"),"export {};\n").unwrap();
    for check in [true,false] {
        let args=if check {vec!["deploy","deployment","--check"]}else{vec!["deploy","deployment"]};
        let out=app.run(&args);assert!(!out.status.success(),"{}",text(&out));
        assert!(text(&out).contains("authored worker bundle"),"{}",text(&out));
        assert_eq!(app.json(&["releases","deployment"])["current"],1);
    }
}
struct App(PathBuf);

#[test]
fn scalar_renamed_unique_keys_keep_their_identity_in_default_rollback() {
    for (kind, value) in [("text <=20", json!("alice")), ("int", json!(7))] {
        for strengthen in [false,true] {
        let app=App::new();
        let old=format!("table items:\n  n: int\n  code: {kind}\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  by byN: n, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return items first 10 by byN\n");
        let old=if strengthen {old.replace("  by byN: n, id", "  by byN: n, id\n  by byCustomer: code")} else {old};
        app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
        app.seed(&old,vec![json!({"id":"kept","n":1,"code":value,"memo":null})]);
        let next=format!("table items:\n  n: int ?\n  label: {kind} renamed from code\n  count: int default 7\n  by byCount: count, label, id\n  unique byCustomer: label\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return items where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount\n");
        app.source(&next);
        app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--check"]);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
        let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);
        let data=app.json(&["sql","SELECT code FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(data["rows"][0][0],value);
        assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);
        let forward=app.json(&["sql","SELECT label FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(forward["rows"][0][0],value);
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn unchanged_quarry_cannot_roll_back_an_executable_only_policy_repair() {
    let app=App::new();
    app.source("table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  public\n  online only\ninvariant docs.read only true\n");
    let worker=app.0.join("snapback/worker.mjs");fs::write(&worker,"export {}; // original\n").unwrap();
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]); // Identical executable remains a no-op.
    assert_eq!(app.json(&["releases","deployment"])["current"],1);
    fs::write(&worker,"export {}; // policy repair with identical Quarry\n").unwrap();
    for check in [true,false] {
        let args=if check {vec!["deploy","deployment","--check"]}else{vec!["deploy","deployment"]};
        let out=app.run(&args);assert!(!out.status.success(),"{}",text(&out));
        assert!(text(&out).contains("authored worker bundle"),"{}",text(&out));
        assert_eq!(app.json(&["releases","deployment"])["current"],1);
    }
}
impl App {
    fn new() -> Self {
        static NEXT: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
        let p = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "sb4-roll-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(p.join("snapback")).unwrap();
        Self(p)
    }
    fn source(&self, s: &str) {
        fs::write(self.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), s).unwrap();
    }
    fn run(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Output {
        Command::new(BIN)
            .args(args)
            .current_dir(&self.0)
            .env_remove("PORT_LO")
            .env_remove("PORT_HI")
            .output()
            .unwrap()
    }
    fn ok(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Output {
        let o = self.run(args);
        assert!(o.status.success(), "{}", text(&o));
        o
    }
    fn json(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Value {
        let o = self.ok(args);
        serde_json::from_slice(&o.stdout).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}: {}", text(&o)))
    }

    /// Evaluate the keys, then choose the index: explicit `by`, else by
    /// key names, count and types. Point reads need a unique full key;
    /// ranges need a strict prefix.
    fn select_index(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>, ast::IndexDecl, String)> {
        // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — retain bound keys for type selection and final lowering.
        // No cloned expression pass: nested lookups must not multiply binder work.
        for key in &read.keys {
            if let Some(field) = &key.column
                && self.schema.tables.get(&read.table).is_some_and(|t|t.column_reads.contains_key(field)) {
                return Err(super::column_reads::refusal(&key.site,format!("{}.{} is protected and cannot supply a lookup key or raw point equality",read.table,field)));
            }
        }
        let mut keys = Vec::<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>::new();
        let mixed = read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_some()) && read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_none());
        let types = if mixed {
            let prefix = read.keys.iter().take_while(|key| key.column.is_none()).count();
            self.bind_key_parts(info, read, prefix, &mut keys, out)?;
            Some(keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>())
        } else { None };
        let mut normalized = super::mixed_keys::read_typed(info, read, self.catalog, types.as_deref())?;
        self.name_bound_keys(info, &normalized, &mut keys)?;
        let read = normalized.as_ref();
        super::validate_key_names(read)?;
        let mut positional_reason = None;
        let mut positional_scan_repair = None;
        if !read.keys.is_empty() && read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_none()) {
            let declared = |index: &&ast::IndexDecl| !self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone()));
            let mut candidates = info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).filter(|index| match &read.index {
                Some(name) => &index.name == name,
                None => index.unique && index.columns.len() == read.keys.len(),
            }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            let mut diagnostic_types = None;
            if read.index.is_none() && candidates.len() > 1 {
                self.bind_key_parts(info, read, read.keys.len(), &mut keys, out)?;
                let types = keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                candidates = super::positional_keys::candidates(info, read, &types, &self.catalog.synthesized);
                diagnostic_types = Some(types);
            }
            // A failed lookup may need operand types to identify a reference
            // from the wrong table. Infer those on a discarded binder/body:
            // successful reads retain the legacy contextual binding path and
            // authored operands are still evaluated exactly once.
            if candidates.is_empty() && diagnostic_types.is_none() {
                let mut probe = self.clone();

                }

                let mut selected = read.clone();
                selected.index = Some(candidates[0].name.clone());

    23	fn declaration(text: &str, line: u32) -> (usize, usize, String) {

    32	    (start, end, text[start..end].to_owned())

   164	            }
   165	            // Constraints are current authority too: carry authored quotas,
   166	            // immutable fields and final-existence requirements verbatim.
   167	            for (_,site) in &old.immutable {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,site){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}
   168	            for cap in &old.caps {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,&cap.site){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}
   169	            for (_,site) in &old.final_exists {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,site){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}
   170	            let at=declaration(text,old.site.line).1;
   171	            for(fields,_)in &next.immutable {edits.push((at,at,format!("  immutable {}\n",fields.join(", "))));}
   172	            for cap in &next.caps {edits.push((at,at,format!("  cap {} by {}\n",cap.max,cap.by.join(", "))));}
   173	            for(expr,_)in &next.final_exists {edits.push((at,at,format!("  after insert <- {}\n",print::expression(&remap(expr)))));}
   174	            for prior in [&old.expire,&old.not_before].into_iter().flatten(){if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,&prior.1){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}
   175	            if let Some((field,_))=&next.expire {edits.push((at,at,format!("  expire at .{field}\n")));}
   176	            if let Some((field,_))=&next.not_before {edits.push((at,at,format!("  not before .{field}\n")));}
   177	            // An authored current endpoint or guard may name a new index. Keep
   178	            // that index with the body; an equal-key old name is not an alias.
   179	            for index in carried_indexes {
   180	                if let Some((_,current_text,_))=docs.iter().find(|(_,_,d)|d.tables.iter().any(|t|t.name==next.name)) {
   181	                    if let Some((a,b))=line_span(&index.site.file,current_text,&index.site){
   182	                        let source=super::release_bridge_indexes::plain_declaration(&index.site.file,&current_text[a..b],index,old,next);
   183	                        edits.push((at,at,format!("{source}\n")));
   184	                        changes.push(format!("{}: retained current named index {} for authored guards and endpoints",old.name,index.name));
   185	                    }
   186	                }
   187	            }
   188	            let mut replaced = BTreeSet::new();
   189	            for rule in &old.rules {
   190	                let Some((start, end)) = line_span(file, text, &rule.site) else {
   191	                    continue;
   192	                };
   193	                if !replaced.insert((start, end)) {
   194	                    continue;
   195	                }
   196	                let rules = old
   197	                    .rules
   198	                    .iter()
   199	                    .filter(|r| r.site == rule.site)
   200	                    .map(|old_rule| {
   201	                        let effect = old_rule.effect.word();
   202	                        match next.rules.iter().find(|r| r.effect == old_rule.effect) {
   203	                            Some(current) => {
   204	                                if let Some(raw)=authored_read(current){return raw}
   205	                                let bindings = if current.bindings.is_empty() {
   206	                                    String::new()
   207	                                } else {
   208	                                    format!(" ({})", current.bindings.join(", "))
   209	                                };
   210	                                format!(
   211	                                    "  {effect}{bindings} <- {}",
   212	                                    print::expression(&remap(&current.value))
   213	                                )
   214	                            }
   215	                            None if old_rule.effect==ast::Effect::Read&&next.public.is_some()=>String::new(),
   216	                            None => format!("  {effect} <- deny"),
   217	                        }
   218	                    })
   219	                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
   220	                    .join("\n");
   221	                if text[start..end] != rules {
   222	                    edits.push((start, end, rules));
   223	                    changes.push(format!(
   224	                        "{}: current authored {} authority retained",
   225	                        old.name,
   226	                        rule.effect.word()
   227	                    ));
   228	                }
   229	            }
   230	            for col in &old.columns {
   231	                let Some(next_col) = next
   232	                    .columns
   233	                    .iter()
   234	                    .find(|c| c.name == col.name || c.renamed_from.as_ref() == Some(&col.name))
   235	                else {
   236	                    continue;
   237	                };
   238	                let grouped = next
   239	                    .column_reads
   240	                    .iter()
   241	                    .find(|(fields, _)| fields.contains(&next_col.name))
   242	                    .map(|(_, r)| &r.value);
   243	                let Some(gate) = next_col.read.as_ref().or(grouped) else {
   244	                    continue;
   245	                };
   246	                if col.read.is_none()
   247	                    && !old
   248	                        .column_reads
   249	                        .iter()
   250	                        .any(|(fields, _)| fields.contains(&col.name))
   251	                {
   252	                    let at = declaration(text, old.site.line).1;
   253	                    edits.push((
   254	                        at,
   255	                        at,
   256	                        format!(
   257	                            "  read({}) <- {}\n",
   258	                            col.name,
   259	                            print::expression(&remap(gate))
   260	                        ),
   261	                    ));
   262	                    changes.push(format!(
   263	                        "{}.{}: retained current payload read guard",
   264	                        old.name, col.name
   265	                    ));
   266	                }
   267	            }
   268	        }
   269	        for old in &doc.programs {
   270	            let mut reads = BTreeSet::new();
   271	            let mut d = ast::Document::default();
   272	            d.programs.push(old.clone());
   273	            crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut d, &mut |e| match e {
   274	                ast::Expr::Read(r)
   275	                | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }
   276	                | ast::Expr::Created { read: r, .. } => {
   277	                    reads.insert(r.table.clone());
   278	                }
   279	                ast::Expr::Write { table, .. } => {
   280	                    reads.insert(table.clone());
   281	                }
   282	                _ => (),
   283	            });
   284	            if reads.is_disjoint(&relaxed)
   285	                && reads.is_disjoint(&expanded)
   286	                && !forced_programs.contains(&super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&old.name))
   287	            {
   288	                continue;
   289	            }
   290	            let Some((current_file, current_text, next)) = programs.get(&super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&old.name)) else {
   291	                continue;
   292	            };
   293	            if old.kind != next.kind
   294	                || old.args.len() != next.args.len()
   295	                || old
   296	                    .args
   297	                    .iter()
   298	                    .zip(&next.args)
   299	                    .any(|(a, b)| a.name != b.name || !input_accepts(&a.type_, &b.type_))
   300	            {
   301	                continue;
   302	            }
   303	            let (_, _, mut source) = declaration(current_text, next.site.line);
   304	            let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(current_file, &source);
   305	            let mut replacements = Vec::new();
   306	            if next.name!=old.name {if let Some(name)=tokens.iter().find(|t|t.value==next.name){let at=offset(&source,name.site.line,name.site.col);replacements.push((at,at+name.value.len(),old.name.clone()));}}
   307	            let parsed = parser::parse_document(current_file, &source).0;
   308	            let mut parsed = parsed;
   309	            if let Some(endpoint)=parsed.programs.first() {
   310	                for prior in &old.args {
   311	                    let Some(alias)=&prior.alias else{continue};
   312	                    let Some(arg)=endpoint.args.iter().find(|arg|arg.name==prior.name&&arg.alias.is_none())else{continue};
   313	                    if let Ok((_,end))=super::release_bridge_current::arg_type(&source,&tokens,arg){replacements.push((end,end,format!(" alias {alias}")));}
   314	                }
   315	            }
   316	            crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut parsed, &mut |e| {
   317	                let (table, site) = match e {
   318	                    ast::Expr::Read(r)
   319	                    | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }
   320	                    | ast::Expr::Created { read: r, .. } => (&r.table, &r.site),
   321	                    ast::Expr::Write { table, site, .. } => (&*table, &*site),
   322	                    _ => return,
   323	                };
   324	                if let Some(old) = aliases.get(table) {
   325	                    if let Some(token) = tokens.iter().find(|t| {
   326	                        t.site.line == site.line && t.site.col >= site.col && t.value == *table
   327	                    }) {
   328	                        let at = offset(&source, token.site.line, token.site.col);
   329	                        replacements.push((at, at + table.len(), old.clone()));
   330	                    }
   331	                }
   332	            });
   333	            replacements.sort();
   334	            replacements.dedup();
   335	            replacements.sort_by_key(|r| std::cmp::Reverse(r.0));
   336	            for (a, b, value) in replacements {
   337	                source.replace_range(a..b, &value);
   338	            }
   339	            let (a, b, _) = declaration(text, old.site.line);
   340	            edits.push((a, b, source));
   341	            changes.push(format!("operation {}: current authored compatibility endpoint preserves its argument contract and guards",super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&old.name)));
   342	        }
   343	        for fill in &doc.backfills {
   344	            let Some(current) = tables.get(&fill.table) else {
   345	                continue;
   346	            };
   347	            if let ast::Expr::Object { fields, .. } = &fill.value {
   348	                if fields
   349	                    .iter()
   350	                    .all(|f| current.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == f.name))
   351	                {
   352	                    let (a, b, _) = declaration(text, fill.site.line);
   353	                    edits.push((a, b, String::new()));
   354	                    changes.push(format!(
   355	                        "{}: retained current forward migration custody instead of historical {}",
   356	                        fill.table, fill.name
   357	                    ));
   358	                }
   359	            }
   360	        }
   361	        for down in &doc.downfills {
   362	            if docs.iter().any(|(_, _, d)| {
   363	                d.downfills
   364	                    .iter()
   365	                    .any(|f| f.table == down.table && f.name == down.name)
   366	            }) {
   367	                continue;
   368	            }
   369	            let Some(current) = tables.get(&down.table) else {
   370	                continue;
   371	            };
   372	            if let ast::Expr::Object { fields, .. } = &down.value {
   373	                if fields
   374	                    .iter()
   375	                    .all(|f| current.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == f.name))
   376	                {
   377	                    let (a, b, _) = declaration(text, down.site.line);
   378	                    edits.push((a, b, String::new()));
   379	                    changes.push(format!("{}: obsolete down {} stays historical; visible view keeps current native values",down.table,down.name));
   380	                }
   381	            }
   382	        }
   383	        for (current_file, current_text, current_doc) in &docs {
   384	            for backfill in &current_doc.backfills {
   385	                if !doc.tables.iter().any(|t| t.name == backfill.table) {
   386	                    continue;
   387	                }
   388	                let (_, _, source) = declaration(current_text, backfill.site.line);
   389	                edits.push((text.len(), text.len(), format!("\n{source}")));
   390	                changes.push(format!(
   391	                    "{}: retained current migration {}",
   392	                    backfill.table, backfill.name
   393	                ));
   394	                let _ = current_file;
   395	            }
   396	        }
   397	        edits.sort_by_key(|e| std::cmp::Reverse((e.0, e.1)));
   398	        for (a, b, value) in edits {
   399	            text.replace_range(a..b, &value);
   400	        }
   401	    }
   402	    relaxed.extend(expanded);
   403	    relaxed
   404	}
   405	
   406	fn current_default(file:&str,text:&str,old:&ast::ColumnDecl,new:&ast::ColumnDecl)->Option<(usize,usize,String)> {
   407	    let authored=|column:&ast::ColumnDecl|column.default.as_ref().map(print::expression);
   408	    if authored(old)==authored(new){return None;}
   409	    let replacement=authored(new).map(|value|format!("default {value}")).unwrap_or_default();
   410	    if let Some(value)=&old.default {
   411	        let(tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,text);
   412	        let at=tokens.iter().position(|token|token.site==*value.site())?;
   413	        let keyword=at.checked_sub(1).filter(|i|tokens[*i].value=="default")?;
   414	        let end=at+if matches!(value,ast::Expr::Unary{..}){2}else{1};
   415	        let end=offset(text,tokens.get(end)?.site.line,tokens.get(end)?.site.col).min(text.len());
   416	        Some((offset(text,tokens[keyword].site.line,tokens[keyword].site.col),text[..end].trim_end().len(),replacement))
   417	    }else{
   418	        let(_,end)=super::release_bridge::type_span(file,text,old)?;
   419	        Some((end,end,format!(" {replacement}")))
   420	    }
   421	}
   422	
   423	pub(super) fn input_accepts(old: &ast::ScalarType, new: &ast::ScalarType) -> bool {
   424	    if old == new {
   425	        return true;
   426	    }
   427	    if old.optional && !new.optional {
   428	        return false;
   429	    }
   430	    use ast::TypeKind::*;
   431	    let bounds = |k: &ast::TypeKind| match k {
   432	        Int => Some((i64::MIN, i64::MAX)),
   433	        IntRange { min, max } => Some((min.unwrap_or(i64::MIN), max.unwrap_or(i64::MAX))),
   434	        _ => None,
   435	    };
   436	    match (bounds(&old.kind), bounds(&new.kind)) {
   437	        (Some((a, b)), Some((x, y))) => x <= a && y >= b,
   438	        _ => match (&old.kind,&new.kind){(Enum{variants:a},Enum{variants:b})=>a.iter().all(|v|b.contains(v)),_=>old.kind==new.kind},
   439	    }
   440	}

   442	#[cfg(test)]
   443	mod tests {
   444	    use crate::release_bridge::loosen;
   445	    #[test]
   446	    fn preserves_current_transition_guard_fields_and_authored_old_client_writes() {
   447	        let old = "table items:\n  n: int 1..10\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(n: int 1..10):\n  return insert items {n}\n";
   448	        let current = "table items:\n  n: int 0..10\n  category: enum('free','paid')\n  insert <- allow\n  update (old,next) <- old.category = next.category\n  online only\nmutation add(n: int 0..10):\n  return insert items {n,category:'free' if n = 0 else 'paid'}\nbackfill items classify(n: int 0..10): {category:'free' if n = 0 else 'paid'}\n";
   449	        let bridge = loosen(
   450	            &[("schema.q".into(), old.into())],
   451	            &[("schema.q".into(), current.into())],
   452	        );
   453	        assert!(
   454	            bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(),
   455	            "{:?}\n{:?}",
   456	            bridge.diagnostics,
   457	            bridge.sources
   458	        );
   459	        let text = &bridge.sources[0].1;
   460	        assert!(text.contains("old.category = next.category"));
   461	        assert!(text.contains("category:'free' if n = 0 else 'paid'"));
   462	        assert!(text.contains("backfill items classify"));
   463	    }
   464	    #[test]
   465	    fn keeps_a_fitting_old_write_when_current_guards_transport_without_replacing_it(){
   466	        let old="table items:\n  amount: int\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add():\n  return insert items {amount:100}\n";
   467	        let current=old.replace("amount: int", "amount: int ?").replace("amount:100", "amount:null");
   468	        let b=loosen(&[("schema.q".into(),old.into())],&[("schema.q".into(),current)]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);assert!(b.sources[0].1.contains("amount:100"));
   469	    }
   470	    #[test]
   471	    fn carries_current_guards_even_when_the_join_has_no_changed_columns(){
   472	        let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(n: int):\n  return insert items {n}\n";
   473	        let current=old.replace("  return insert items","  require n > 0 else TOO_EARLY\n  return insert items");
   474	        let b=loosen(&[("schema.q".into(),old.into())],&[("schema.q".into(),current)]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);assert!(b.sources[0].1.contains("TOO_EARLY"));
   475	    }
   476	    #[test]
   477	    fn does_not_reuse_an_endpoint_that_requires_new_arguments() {
   478	        let old = "table items:\n  n: int\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(n: int):\n  return insert items {n}\n";
   479	        let current = "table items:\n  n: int\n  required: int\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(n: int, required: int):\n  return insert items {n,required}\n";
   480	        let bridge = loosen(
   481	            &[("schema.q".into(), old.into())],
   482	            &[("schema.q".into(), current.into())],
   483	        );
   484	        assert!(!bridge.diagnostics.is_empty());
   485	    }
   486	}

   124	
   125	pub(super) fn contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound)->bool {
   126	    table_contracts(old,new,false)&&endpoint_contracts(old,new,false)
   127	}
   128	fn table_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,nullable_gaps:bool)->bool {
   129	    old.schema.tables.iter().all(|(name,table)|new.schema.tables.get(name).is_some_and(|next| {
   130	        table.columns.iter().all(|(column,kind)|next.columns.contains_key(column)
   131	            || (nullable_gaps&&renamed_column(old,new,name,column).is_some())
   132	            || (nullable_gaps&&kind.is_optional()&&!table.defaults.contains_key(column)&&!table.engine_field(column)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(column)))
   133	    }))
   134	}
   135	fn endpoint_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,pause_removed_writes:bool)->bool {
   136	    old.programs.iter().all(|p|new.programs.iter().find(|n|n.program.name==p.program.name)
   137	        .map_or_else(||pause_removed_writes&&matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action),|n|endpoint_contract(&p.program,&n.program)))
   138	}

   141	    let mut positions=BTreeSet::new();
   142	    for(name,kind)in &old.args {

            if read.index.is_none() && mode == ReadMode::Range && read.order_keys.is_empty() && candidates.len() == 1 {
                let scan = info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).find(|index| !index.unique && index.is_plain() && index.columns.len() > read.keys.len());
                let columns = scan.map(|index| index.columns.as_slice()).unwrap_or(&candidates[0].columns);
                let predicate = read.keys.iter().zip(columns).map(|(key, column)| format!(".{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
                positional_scan_repair = Some(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN", "a positional key selects one declared unique row, not a scan prefix",
                    format!("{} where {predicate} first 20 by .id", read.table)).guide("reads"));
            }
            if candidates.len() != 1 {
                return Err(super::positional_keys::refusal(info, read, diagnostic_types.as_deref().unwrap_or(&[]), &candidates, self.catalog));
            }
            if read.index.is_none() {
                positional_reason = Some(if info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).filter(|index| index.unique && index.columns.len() == read.keys.len()).count() == 1 {
                    "only declared unique key of this arity"
                } else { "unique positional key selected by argument types" });
                normalized.to_mut().index = Some(candidates[0].name.clone());
            }
        }
        let read = normalized.as_ref();
        let point = mode != ReadMode::Range;
        self.bind_key_parts(info, read, read.keys.len(), &mut keys, out)?;
        let named = keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| c.is_some()) && !keys.is_empty();
        let mut selection = if read.index.is_some() { "explicit by" } else if named { "named equality columns" } else { "compatible positional key types" };
        // A `words(..)` index serves one read: `matching` with exactly its
        // leading columns as keys. Every other read sees plain indexes only.
        let searching = mode == ReadMode::Range && read.matching.is_some();
        let mut candidates: Vec<&ast::IndexDecl> = info
            .indexes
            .iter()
            .filter(|index| read.index.as_ref().is_none_or(|explicit| explicit == &index.name))
            .filter(|index| read.seeks.iter().all(|b| index.columns.get(keys.len()) == Some(&b.column)))
            .filter(|index| if searching { index.searches_after(keys.len()) } else { index.words.is_none() })
            .filter(|index| match mode {
                ReadMode::Point | ReadMode::FilteredPoint => index.unique && (index.columns.len() == keys.len() || (named && mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() < keys.len())),
                ReadMode::Exists => (index.columns.len() == keys.len() && (named || read.index.is_some() || index.unique)) || (named && info.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() < keys.len()),
                ReadMode::Range => index.columns.len() > keys.len() || (!read.order_keys.is_empty() && index.unique && index.columns.len() == keys.len()),
            })
            .filter(|index| {
                if named {
                    if index.columns.len() < keys.len() { index.columns.iter().all(|column| keys.iter().any(|(c, _, _)| c.as_ref() == Some(column))) } else { keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| index.columns[..keys.len()].contains(c.as_ref().unwrap())) }
                } else {
                    true
                }
            })
            .collect();
        if named && matches!(mode, ReadMode::FilteredPoint | ReadMode::Exists) {
            if let Some(width) = candidates.iter().filter(|i| info.total_unique(i)).map(|i| i.columns.len()).min() {
                candidates.retain(|i| info.total_unique(i) && i.columns.len() == width);

//! A positional unique key followed by named tests is named-key sugar.
// @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — only declared unique keys resolve positions; extra equalities test one row.
use super::{catalog::{Catalog, TableInfo}, intent_walk as walk};
use crate::{ast::*, diag::{Diagnostic, Fallible, Site}, print};
use std::{borrow::Cow, collections::BTreeMap};

pub(super) fn keys<'a>(table: &TableInfo, parts: &'a [KeyPart], index: Option<&str>, site: &Site, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>, types: Option<&[(crate::types::Ty, bool)]>) -> Fallible<Cow<'a, [KeyPart]>> {
    if !parts.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_some()) || !parts.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_none()) { return Ok(Cow::Borrowed(parts)); }
    let prefix = parts.iter().take_while(|key| key.column.is_none()).count();
    if let Some(late) = parts[prefix..].iter().find(|key| key.column.is_none()) {
        let ordered = parts.iter().filter(|key| key.column.is_none()).chain(parts.iter().filter(|key| key.column.is_some())).map(spelling).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        return Err(Diagnostic::new(&late.site, "E_QUARRY_KEY_ORDER", "positional key components come before named column tests",
            format!("{}[{ordered}]", table.decl.name)).guide("reads"));
    }
    let declared: Vec<_> = table.indexes.iter().filter(|key| key.unique
        && !synthesized.contains(&(table.decl.name.clone(), key.name.clone()))).collect();
    let candidates: Vec<_> = if let Some(types) = types {
        let read = ReadExpr { table: table.decl.name.clone(), keys: parts[..prefix].to_vec(), index: index.map(str::to_owned), site: site.clone(),
            cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new() };
        super::positional_keys::candidates(table, &read, types, synthesized).into_iter()
            .filter(|key| key.unique && key.columns.len() == prefix).collect()
    } else {
        // Before types are known, do not silently turn `roles[viewer,
        // role = 'staff']` into an id lookup merely because byId has the
        // positional prefix's arity. A declared composite key covered by the
        // positional prefix plus named suffix must reach the typed pass, which
        // prints the fully named repair.
        let named: Vec<&str> = parts[prefix..].iter().filter_map(|part| part.column.as_deref()).collect();
        let partial_composite = declared.iter().any(|key| key.columns.len() > prefix && key.columns.len() <= parts.len()
            && key.columns[prefix..].iter().all(|column| named.contains(&column.as_str()))
            && index.is_none_or(|name| name == key.name));
        if partial_composite { Vec::new() }
        else { declared.iter().copied().filter(|key| key.columns.len() == prefix && index.is_none_or(|name| name == key.name)).collect() }
    };
    let chosen = match candidates.as_slice() {
        [chosen] if table.total_unique(chosen) => *chosen,
        _ => {
            let suggested = candidates.first().copied().or_else(|| declared.iter().copied().find(|key| key.columns.len() > prefix)).or_else(|| declared.first().copied());
            let descriptions = declared.iter().map(|key| format!("({})", key.columns.join(", "))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" or ");
            let rewrite = if let Some(key) = suggested {
                let mut named = key.columns.iter().enumerate().map(|(at, column)| {
                    let value = parts.get(at).filter(|_| at < prefix).map(|part| print::expression(&part.value))
                        .or_else(|| parts.iter().find(|part| part.column.as_ref() == Some(column)).map(|part| print::expression(&part.value)))
                        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("<{column}>"));
                    format!("{column} = {value}")
                }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                named.extend(parts[prefix..].iter().filter(|part| !key.columns.contains(part.column.as_ref().unwrap())).map(spelling));
                format!("{}[{}]", table.decl.name, named.join(", "))
            } else { format!("{}[id = <id>, {}]", table.decl.name, parts[prefix..].iter().map(spelling).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")) };
            return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN",
                format!("{} does not have a full positional unique key of non-null columns before its named tests; do not mix positional and named keys in this read; use the fully named replacement; {prefix} positional components match {} keys; declared keys: {descriptions}", table.decl.name, candidates.len()), rewrite.clone())
                .source_rewrite(crate::diag::SourceRewriteKind::MixedKey, rewrite).guide("reads"));
        }
    };
    let mut named = parts.to_vec();
    for (part, column) in named.iter_mut().zip(&chosen.columns) { part.column = Some(column.clone()); }
    Ok(Cow::Owned(named))
}

fn spelling(part: &KeyPart) -> String {
    match &part.column { Some(column) => format!("{column} = {}", print::expression(&part.value)), None => print::expression(&part.value) }
}

pub(super) fn read<'a>(table: &TableInfo, read: &'a ReadExpr, catalog: &Catalog) -> Fallible<Cow<'a, ReadExpr>> {
    read_typed(table, read, catalog, None)
}

pub(super) fn read_typed<'a>(table: &TableInfo, read: &'a ReadExpr, catalog: &Catalog, types: Option<&[(crate::types::Ty, bool)]>) -> Fallible<Cow<'a, ReadExpr>> {
    Ok(match keys(table, &read.keys, read.index.as_deref(), &read.site, &catalog.synthesized, types)? {
        Cow::Borrowed(_) => Cow::Borrowed(read),
        Cow::Owned(keys) => {
            let index = types.and_then(|types| super::positional_keys::candidates(table, read, types, &catalog.synthesized)
                .first().map(|index| index.name.clone())).or_else(|| read.index.clone());
            Cow::Owned(ReadExpr { keys, index, ..read.clone() })
        },
    })
}

/// Normalize before planning and sync inference, including helpers and shapes.
/// Invalid keys remain for the ordinary binder to diagnose at their use site.
pub(super) fn normalize(catalog: &mut Catalog) {
    let tables: BTreeMap<_, _> = catalog.tables.clone();
    let mut visit = |value: &mut Expr| {
        let (name, parts, index, site) = match value {
            Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } => (&read.table, &mut read.keys, read.index.as_deref(), &read.site),
            Expr::Write { table, keys, site, .. } => (&*table, keys, None, &*site),
            _ => return,
        };
        if let Some(table) = tables.get(name)
            && let Ok(Cow::Owned(named)) = keys(table, parts, index, site, &catalog.synthesized, None) { *parts = named; }
    };
    for table in catalog.tables.values_mut() {
        for rule in &mut table.decl.rules { walk::expression(&mut rule.value, &mut visit); }
        for (value, _) in &mut table.decl.final_exists { walk::expression(value, &mut visit); }
    }
    for doc in &mut catalog.documents {
        walk::document(doc, &mut visit);
        for view in &mut doc.views { for branch in &mut view.branches {
            if let Some(predicate) = &mut branch.predicate { walk::expression(predicate, &mut visit); }
            walk::shape(&mut branch.fields, &mut visit);
        } }
    }
    catalog.shapes = catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| doc.shapes.iter().cloned().map(|shape| (shape.name.clone(), shape))).collect();
    catalog.functions = catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| doc.functions.iter().cloned().map(|function| (function.name.clone(), function))).collect();
}

//! Bind key operands once, retaining authored sequencing and contextual literals.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    pub(super) fn bind_key_parts(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, end: usize,
        keys: &mut Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<()> {
        for (position, part) in read.keys.iter().enumerate().take(end).skip(keys.len()) {
            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
            let expected = part.column.as_deref().or_else(|| read.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == name)).and_then(|i| i.columns.get(position)).map(String::as_str)).and_then(|column| key_identity(info, column));
            let (e, t) = match expected {
                Some(expected) => self.expr_as_reference(&part.value, &mut setup, &expected)?,
                None => self.expr(&part.value, &mut setup)?,
            };
            self.sequence_setup(keys.iter_mut().map(|(_, e, _)| e), setup, out);
            let e = if expr::allocates(&e) { self.snapshot(e, out) } else { e };
            keys.push((part.column.clone(), e, t));
        }
        self.contextualize_bound_keys(info, read, keys)?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Apply the selected index's identity context to operands already bound
    /// for type-directed positional selection. This changes only their
    /// retained IR/type and never evaluates the authored expression again.
    pub(super) fn contextualize_bound_keys(&self, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr,
        keys: &mut [(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)]) -> Fallible<()> {
        if let Some(index) = read.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == name)) {
            for (position,(column,value,ty)) in keys.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                if let Some(expected) = column.as_deref().or_else(|| index.columns.get(position).map(String::as_str)).and_then(|c| key_identity(info,c)) {
                    if let Expr::Literal(Value::String(text)) = value {
                        if text.is_empty() {
                            return Err(type_error(read.keys[position].value.site(), "an empty text literal cannot be an id", "use a non-empty id literal, or null"));
                        }
                        *value = Expr::Literal(Value::Id(text.clone()));
                        *ty = expected.required();
                    } else {
                        (*value,*ty) = self.row_identity(value.clone(),ty.clone(),&expected);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    // Mixed-key normalization names an already-bound positional prefix. Only
    // a literal acquires new context; compound expressions keep their IR/setup.
    pub(super) fn name_bound_keys(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr,
        keys: &mut [(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)]) -> Fallible<()> {
        for ((column, expr, ty), part) in keys.iter_mut().zip(&read.keys) {
            *column = part.column.clone();
            if let Some(expected) = column.as_deref().and_then(|column| key_identity(info, column))
                && matches!(&part.value, A::Literal { value: Literal::Str(_), .. }) {
                (*expr, *ty) = self.expr_as_reference(&part.value, &mut setup::Body::default(), &expected)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn key_identity(info: &TableInfo, column: &str) -> Option<Ty> {
    let ty = if column == "id" { Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone()) }
        else { Ty::from_scalar(info.columns.get(column)?) };
    ty.required().is_identity().then_some(ty)
}

    /// A heap-bound allocation can be written; the row validator checks its
    /// actual length. It cannot claim a smaller bound inside an inline helper.
    pub(crate) fn writable_to(&self, expected: &Ty) -> bool {
        match (self, expected) {
            (Ty::Omittable(a),b) => a.as_ref().clone().optional().writable_to(b),
            (a,Ty::Omittable(b)) => a.writable_to(b),
            (Ty::Optional(a), Ty::Optional(b)) => a.writable_to(b),
            (a, Ty::Optional(b)) => a.writable_to(b) || *a == Ty::Null,
            (Ty::Text(u32::MAX), Ty::Text(_)) => true,
            (Ty::List(a, am), Ty::List(b, bm)) => a.writable_to(b) && (*am == u32::MAX || am <= bm),
            _ => self.assignable_to(expected),
        }
    }

   660	                    diagnostic_types = Some(probe_keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect());
   661	                }
   662	            }
   663	            if candidates.len() == 1 && !keys.is_empty() {
   664	                let mut selected = read.clone();
   665	                selected.index = Some(candidates[0].name.clone());
   666	                self.contextualize_bound_keys(info, &selected, &mut keys)?;
   667	            }
   668	            if read.index.is_none() && mode == ReadMode::Range && read.order_keys.is_empty() && candidates.len() == 1 {
   669	                let scan = info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).find(|index| !index.unique && index.is_plain() && index.columns.len() > read.keys.len());
   670	                let columns = scan.map(|index| index.columns.as_slice()).unwrap_or(&candidates[0].columns);
   671	                let predicate = read.keys.iter().zip(columns).map(|(key, column)| format!(".{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");

   673	                    format!("{} where {predicate} first 20 by .id", read.table)).guide("reads"));
   674	            }
   675	            if candidates.len() != 1 {
   676	                return Err(super::positional_keys::refusal(info, read, diagnostic_types.as_deref().unwrap_or(&[]), &candidates, self.catalog));
   677	            }

codex
MATERIAL — ordinary unique-index renaming can falsely refuse deployment.

Location: [release_bridge_rules.rs:92](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/release_bridge_rules.rs:92).

Concrete counterexample, with an empty `retired` table:

```quarry
table notes:
  n: int
  code: text <=20
  unique byCode: code
  public
  online only
table retired:
  value: int
  public
  online only
query lookup(code: text <=20):
  return notes[code]
```

For v2, make only these changes:

- Widen `n` to `int ?`.
- Rename index `byCode` to `byCurrentCode`, retaining key `code`.
- Remove `retired`.

The widening requires a rollback view. Transport inserts `byCurrentCode`, but retains historical `byCode`: `loses_unique` is false because current still has uniqueness over `code`, and removal otherwise matches names.

The generated view therefore contains two equivalent declared unique keys. Its unchanged `notes[code]` lookup becomes ambiguous and receives `E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN` at [program.rs:675](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program.rs:675). Both current-source fallbacks fail because [table_contracts](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/release_bridge_current.rs:128) requires the removed predecessor table. Thus valid ordinary evolution refuses solely because transport introduced redundant lookup candidates.

The existing equal-key/different-name regression uses an explicit index and misses this positional-lookup case.

This is a static source-traced counterexample; I ran no builds or tests and wrote nothing. The supplied revision string does not resolve; reviewed final checkout `024f978514349c231c802c1e7c98c840263738f4` and the four correction diffs.
